=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/orchestrator/RetryLoop.ts ===
// RetryLoop - "生成→验证→自修复"闭环
// 每个 SA 步骤都用这个循环跑

import { ValidationError } from '../types';
import { ISADatabase, SAContext, ValidationLogRecord } from '../types';

export interface RetryLoopConfig {
  maxRetries: number;
  retryDelayMs: number;
}

export interface RetryResult<T> {
  output: T;
  attempts: number;
  validationPassed: boolean;
  errors: ValidationError[];
}

/**
 * 生成 → 验证 → 错误回灌 → 重试
 */
export async function runWithRetry<T>(
  stepName: string,
  ctx: SAContext,
  db: ISADatabase,
  config: RetryLoopConfig,
  generate: () => Promise<T>,
  validate: (output: T) => { passed: boolean; errors: ValidationError[] }
): Promise<RetryResult<T>> {
  let lastOutput: T;
  let lastResult: { passed: boolean; errors: ValidationError[] } = { passed: false, errors: [] };

  for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= config.maxRetries; attempt++) {
    const startTime = Date.now();

    // Step 1: LLM 生成
    lastOutput = await generate();

    // Step 2: Validator 校验
    lastResult = validate(lastOutput);
    const duration = Date.now() - startTime;

    // Step 3: 写 sa_validation_log(含重试闭环)
    const logRecord: ValidationLogRecord = {
      tenantId: ctx.tenantId,
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      saTableName: stepName,
      validatorName: 'default',
      retryCount: attempt - 1,
      previousErrors: ctx.lastErrors || null,
      isConverged: lastResult.passed,
      validationStatus: lastResult.passed ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL',
      errors: lastResult.errors,
      durationMs: duration,
    };
    await db.logValidation(logRecord);

    if (lastResult.passed) {
      return {
        output: lastOutput!,
        attempts: attempt,
        validationPassed: true,
        errors: [],
      };
    }

    // Step 4: 错误回灌到 ctx.lastErrors(下一轮 LLM 看到)
    ctx.lastErrors = lastResult.errors.map(e => `[${e.code}] ${e.message}`);
    console.warn(`[${stepName}] Attempt ${attempt}/${config.maxRetries} failed:`, lastResult.errors.length, 'errors');

    // 等待重试延迟(避免过快打 LLM)
    if (attempt < config.maxRetries) {
      await sleep(config.retryDelayMs);
    }
  }

  // 所有重试都失败
  throw new Error(
    `[${stepName}] 失败 ${config.maxRetries} 次仍未收敛。最后错误:\n` +
    (lastResult?.errors || []).map(e => `  [${e.code}] ${e.message}`).join('\n')
  );
}

function sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {
  return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

=== END FILE:  ===

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/orchestrator/SAOrchestrator.ts ===
// SAOrchestrator - 重写版
// ★ 关键改进:
// 1. Phase 1 串行链(DFD→BPM→Dict)后,ER/STD 并行(省 5 秒)
// 2. Phase 2/3 60 事件用 Semaphore(并发 5),省 10 分钟
// 3. 每个事件 fork 独立 ctx(避免 previousSteps 污染)
// 4. 事件失败隔离:单事件异常不阻断其他事件
// 5. 不在输出里覆盖:用 Map<eventId, EventAssets>
// 6. 完整返回 9 步(包括 PSpec/DecisionTable/UI)

import {
  SARequest, SAContext, SAOutput, SAConfig, DEFAULT_SA_CONFIG,
  ScopeOutput, DFDOutput, BPMOutput, DictOutput, PSpecOutput,
  DecisionTableOutput, EROutput, StateMachineOutput, UIOutput,
  ISADatabase, ILLMClient, ValidationError, SAProgressEvent, KGPattern,
} from '../types';
import { randomUUID } from 'crypto';
const uuidv4 = () => randomUUID();
import type { BusinessEvent, EventAssets } from './StepRouter';
import {
  ScopeAgent, DFDAgent, BPMAgent, DictAgent, PSpecAgent,
  DecisionTableAgent, ERAgent, StateMachineAgent, UIAgent,
} from '../agents';
import { runWithRetry, RetryResult } from './RetryLoop';
import { runScopeStep, classifyEvent } from './StepRouter';
import type { IndustryType } from '../dkee/PatternTypes';
import { Semaphore } from './Semaphore';
import { DKEEFacade } from '../dkee';

export interface ValidatorBundle {
  DFDValidator: any;
  BPMValidator: any;
  DictValidator: any;
  LogicValidator: any;
  CrossEventConsistencyValidator: any;
  ERValidator: any;
  STDReachabilityValidator: any;
  UIValidator: any;
}

export const DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENCY = 5;

export class SAOrchestrator {
  private agents: Map<string, any>;
  private dkee: DKEEFacade;
  private sem: Semaphore;

  constructor(
    private llm: ILLMClient,
    public db: ISADatabase,
    private validators: ValidatorBundle,
    private config: SAConfig = DEFAULT_SA_CONFIG,
    maxConcurrency: number = DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENCY,
  ) {
    this.agents = new Map<string, any>([
      ['ScopeAgent', new ScopeAgent(llm)],
      ['DFDAgent', new DFDAgent(llm)],
      ['BPMAgent', new BPMAgent(llm)],
      ['DictAgent', new DictAgent(llm)],
      ['PSpecAgent', new PSpecAgent(llm)],
      ['DecisionTableAgent', new DecisionTableAgent(llm)],
      ['ERAgent', new ERAgent(llm)],
      ['StateMachineAgent', new StateMachineAgent(llm)],
      ['UIAgent', new UIAgent(llm)],
    ]);
    this.dkee = new DKEEFacade();
    this.sem = new Semaphore(maxConcurrency);
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 主入口:3-Tier 并行执行
  // ★ 关键:每次 runSA() 生成唯一 pipelineId,每步设 pipelineStep
  // 写入 DB 的每条记录都带这 2 个字段,可追溯、可增量、可重放
  // ============================================================
  async runSA(req: SARequest, onProgress?: (e: SAProgressEvent) => void): Promise<SAOutput> {
    const startTime = Date.now();
    const validationStats: any[] = [];

    // ★ 本次 pipeline 的唯一标识(全 9 步所有记录都用这个)
    const pipelineId = uuidv4();

    // 默认 noop emitter - 不持有外部回调引用(防内存泄漏)
    let progressFn = onProgress;
    if (!progressFn) {
      progressFn = () => {};
    }
    const emit = (event: SAProgressEvent) => {
      try { progressFn!(event); } catch { /* ignore listener errors */ }
    };

    emit({ type: 'started', projectId: req.projectId, assetLevel: req.assetLevel || 'PROJECT', pipelineId });
    console.log(`[SA] start pipelineId=${pipelineId} project=${req.projectId} tenant=${req.tenantId}`);

    // 解析 Context
    const ctx = await this.resolveContext(req);
    ctx.assetLevel = 'PROJECT';  // Phase 1 强制 PROJECT
    (ctx as any).pipelineId = pipelineId;  // ★ 注入 pipelineId

    // ===========================================
    // Phase 0: Scope(串行,入口)
    // ===========================================
    (ctx as any).pipelineStep = 1;
    (ctx as any).stepName = 'Scope';
    const t0 = Date.now();
    const scope = await runScopeStep(this, ctx);
    ctx.previousSteps['scope'] = scope;
    validationStats.push({ step: 'Scope', attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t0 });
    emit({ type: 'step_done', step: 'Scope', eventCount: scope.eventCount });

    // ===========================================
    // Phase 1: Project-level(部分并行)
    // 串行链: DFD → BPM → Dict(3 步必须串)
    // 并行组: ER + STD(都只依赖 Dict)
    // ===========================================
    const projectT0 = Date.now();
    const { dfd, bpm, dict, er, stateMachine } = await this.runProjectPhase(ctx, emit, validationStats);
    ctx.projectDict = dict;  // ★ 保存为 Project 级全局字典(Event 级复用)
    console.log(`[SA] Phase 1 done in ${Date.now() - projectT0}ms`);

    // 加载已存在的判定表(供 cross-event 一致性检查)
    const existingDecisionTables = await this.loadExistingDecisionTables(ctx);

    // ===========================================
    // Phase 2/3: Per-event 并行(Semaphore 限制并发数)
    // 60 事件 × 平均 3 步 = 180 LLM calls
    // 并发 5 = 36 batches × ~3s = ~108s(对比串行 540s)
    // ===========================================
    const eventT0 = Date.now();
    const eventAssets = new Map<number, EventAssets>();
    const eventErrors = new Map<number, string>();
    const eventDurations = new Map<number, number>();

    // 并行处理所有事件,但用 Semaphore 限制 LLM 并发
    await Promise.all(
      scope.businessEvents.map((event: BusinessEvent) =>
        this.sem.run(async () => {
          const eT0 = Date.now();
          // Fork 独立 ctx(防止 previousSteps 污染)
          const eventCtx = this.forkEventContext(ctx, event.id, dict, existingDecisionTables);
          try {
            const assets = await this.processEvent(event, eventCtx, emit, validationStats);
            eventAssets.set(event.id, assets);
          } catch (err) {
            eventErrors.set(event.id, (err as Error).message);
            console.error(`[SA] event "${event.name}" (id=${event.id}) failed:`, (err as Error).message);
            validationStats.push({ step: `Event_${event.id}`, attempts: 0, passed: false, error: (err as Error).message });
          }
          eventDurations.set(event.id, Date.now() - eT0);
        })
      )
    );
    console.log(`[SA] Phase 2/3 done in ${Date.now() - eventT0}ms (${scope.businessEvents.length} events)`);

    // ===========================================
    // Phase 4: DKEE 提炼
    // ===========================================
    if (this.config.enableDKEE) {
      emit({ type: 'dkee_start' });
      const industry = this.inferIndustry(ctx.requirementText);
      await this.dkee.extractAndScore(industry as IndustryType);
      emit({ type: 'dkee_done' });
    }

    const totalDuration = Date.now() - startTime;
    console.log(`[SA] all done in ${totalDuration}ms, success=${eventErrors.size === 0 ? 'all' : `${eventAssets.size}/${eventErrors.size + eventAssets.size}`}`);

    // 构造完整 9 步返回
    // 兼容旧版:PSpec/DecisionTable/UI 也通过 single 字段暴露(取第一个事件)
    // 推荐使用 eventAssets Map 查具体事件
    const sampleAssets: EventAssets = eventAssets.values().next().value || {} as any;

    const output: SAOutput = {
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      tenantId: ctx.tenantId,
      scope,
      dfd, bpm, dict,
      pspec: sampleAssets.pspec,                    // 兼容旧 API
      decisionTable: sampleAssets.decisionTable,
      er, stateMachine,
      ui: sampleAssets.ui,
      eventAssets,                                   // ★ 新字段:每事件独立
      eventErrors,                                   // ★ 新字段:失败的事件
      metadata: {
        totalDuration,
        totalRetries: 0,
        validationStats,
        eventCount: scope.businessEvents.length,
        eventSuccessCount: eventAssets.size,
        eventErrorCount: eventErrors.size,
        concurrency: this.sem.stats,
      },
    };

    emit({ type: 'all_done', totalDuration });

    return output;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Phase 1: Project-level(部分并行,3+2 步)
  // ============================================================
  private async runProjectPhase(
    ctx: SAContext,
    emit: (e: SAProgressEvent) => void,
    stats: any[],
  ): Promise<{ dfd: DFDOutput; bpm: BPMOutput; dict: DictOutput; er: EROutput; stateMachine: StateMachineOutput }> {
    // 串行:DFD (Step 2)
    (ctx as any).pipelineStep = 2;
    (ctx as any).stepName = 'DFD';
    emit({ type: 'step_start', step: 'DFD' });
    const t1 = Date.now();
    const dfd = await this.runStepWithValidation<DFDOutput>('DFDAgent', 'sa_dfd', ctx,
      async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveDFD(output, ctx, ctx.scopeId!); ctx.dfdId = id; });
    ctx.previousSteps['dfd'] = dfd;
    stats.push({ step: 'DFD', attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t1 });
    emit({ type: 'step_done', step: 'DFD' });

    // 串行:BPM (Step 3)
    (ctx as any).pipelineStep = 3;
    (ctx as any).stepName = 'BPM';
    emit({ type: 'step_start', step: 'BPM' });
    const t2 = Date.now();
    const bpm = await this.runStepWithValidation<BPMOutput>('BPMAgent', 'sa_business_process', ctx,
      async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveBPM(output, ctx, ctx.dfdId!); ctx.bpmId = id; });
    ctx.previousSteps['bpm'] = bpm;
    stats.push({ step: 'BPM', attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t2 });
    emit({ type: 'step_done', step: 'BPM' });

    // 串行:Dict (Step 4) - 关键,后续步骤都依赖它
    (ctx as any).pipelineStep = 4;
    (ctx as any).stepName = 'Dict';
    emit({ type: 'step_start', step: 'Dict' });
    const t3 = Date.now();
    const dict = await this.runStepWithValidation<DictOutput>('DictAgent', 'sa_data_dictionary', ctx,
      async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveDict(output, ctx, ctx.dfdId!, ctx.bpmId!); ctx.dictId = id; });
    ctx.previousSteps['dict'] = dict;
    stats.push({ step: 'Dict', attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t3 });
    emit({ type: 'step_done', step: 'Dict' });

    // ★ 并行:ER (Step 7) + STD (Step 8) - 都只依赖 Dict
    // Step 5 PSpec / Step 6 DT 是 EVENT/PROCESS 级,这里不跑
    (ctx as any).pipelineStep = 7;
    (ctx as any).stepName = 'ER';
    (ctx as any).pipelineStep = 8;
    (ctx as any).stepName = 'StateMachine';
    emit({ type: 'step_start', step: 'ER+STD' });
    const [er, stateMachine] = await Promise.all([
      this.runStepWithValidation<EROutput>('ERAgent', 'sa_er', ctx,
        async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveER(output, ctx, ctx.dictId!); ctx.erId = id; }),
      this.runStepWithValidation<StateMachineOutput>('StateMachineAgent', 'sa_state_machine', ctx,
        async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveStateMachine(output, ctx, ctx.dictId!, ctx.bpmId!); ctx.stateMachineId = id; }),
    ]);
    ctx.previousSteps['er'] = er;
    ctx.previousSteps['stateMachine'] = stateMachine;
    stats.push({ step: 'ER', attempts: 1, passed: true });
    stats.push({ step: 'STD', attempts: 1, passed: true });
    emit({ type: 'step_done', step: 'ER+STD' });

    return { dfd, bpm, dict, er, stateMachine };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Phase 2/3: 单事件处理(根据 6 问清单决定跑哪些 Agent)
  // ============================================================
  private async processEvent(
    event: BusinessEvent,
    eventCtx: SAContext,
    emit: (e: SAProgressEvent) => void,
    stats: any[],
  ): Promise<EventAssets> {
    const decision = classifyEvent(event, eventCtx.projectDict);
    eventCtx.assetLevel = decision.assetLevel;
    eventCtx.currentEventId = event.id;

    console.log(`[SA] event "${event.name}" → ${decision.assetLevel} (${decision.reason})`);

    const assets: EventAssets = {
      eventId: event.id,
      assetLevel: decision.assetLevel,
    };

    // 按依赖顺序跑(避免不必要的依赖等待)
    // 1. PSpec(可能依赖 Dict)
    if (decision.stepsToRun.includes('PSpecAgent')) {
      emit({ type: 'event_step_start', step: 'PSpec', eventId: event.id });
      const t = Date.now();
      assets.pspec = await this.runStepWithValidation<PSpecOutput>('PSpecAgent', 'sa_pspec', eventCtx,
        async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.savePSpec(output, eventCtx, eventCtx.dictId!, eventCtx.bpmId!); eventCtx.pspecId = id; });
      stats.push({ step: `Event_${event.id}_PSpec`, attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t });
      emit({ type: 'event_step_done', step: 'PSpec', eventId: event.id });
    }

    // 2. DecisionTable(可能依赖 PSpec + Dict + 已有 DT)
    if (decision.stepsToRun.includes('DecisionTableAgent')) {
      emit({ type: 'event_step_start', step: 'DecisionTable', eventId: event.id });
      const t = Date.now();
      assets.decisionTable = await this.runStepWithValidation<DecisionTableOutput>('DecisionTableAgent', 'sa_decision_table', eventCtx,
        async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveDecisionTable(output, eventCtx, eventCtx.pspecId || 0, eventCtx.dictId!); eventCtx.decisionTableId = id; });
      stats.push({ step: `Event_${event.id}_DT`, attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t });
      emit({ type: 'event_step_done', step: 'DecisionTable', eventId: event.id });
    }

    // 3. StateMachine(可能依赖 Dict + BPM)
    if (decision.stepsToRun.includes('StateMachineAgent')) {
      emit({ type: 'event_step_start', step: 'StateMachine', eventId: event.id });
      const t = Date.now();
      (assets as any).stateMachine = await this.runStepWithValidation<StateMachineOutput>('StateMachineAgent', 'sa_state_machine', eventCtx,
        async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveStateMachine(output, eventCtx, eventCtx.dictId!, eventCtx.bpmId!); });
      stats.push({ step: `Event_${event.id}_STD`, attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t });
      emit({ type: 'event_step_done', step: 'StateMachine', eventId: event.id });
    }

    // 4. UI(总跑)
    if (decision.stepsToRun.includes('UIAgent')) {
      emit({ type: 'event_step_start', step: 'UI', eventId: event.id });
      const t = Date.now();
      assets.ui = await this.runStepWithValidation<UIOutput>('UIAgent', 'sa_ui', eventCtx,
        async (output) => { const { id } = await this.db.saveUI(output, eventCtx, eventCtx.bpmId!, eventCtx.dictId!); });

      stats.push({ step: `Event_${event.id}_UI`, attempts: 1, passed: true, durationMs: Date.now() - t });
      emit({ type: 'event_step_done', step: 'UI', eventId: event.id });
    }

    return assets;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // Fork 独立 ctx(防止 previousSteps 跨事件污染)
  // ★ 不共享引用 - 深拷贝必要字段,共享不变字段
  // ============================================================
  private forkEventContext(
    parent: SAContext,
    eventId: number,
    projectDict: DictOutput,
    existingDecisionTables: any[],
  ): SAContext {
    return {
      tenantId: parent.tenantId,
      projectId: parent.projectId,
      requirementId: parent.requirementId,
      requirementText: parent.requirementText,
      eventId,
      eventDescription: parent.eventDescription,
      assetLevel: 'EVENT',
      // ★ 继承 Phase 1 的中间 ID(供 DB save 时强外键用)
      scopeId: parent.scopeId,
      dfdId: parent.dfdId,
      bpmId: parent.bpmId,
      dictId: parent.dictId,
      erId: parent.erId,
      stateMachineId: parent.stateMachineId,
      // pspecId/decisionTableId/uiId 不继承(事件独立)
      // ★ 深拷贝 kgPatterns,防止修改
      kgPatterns: parent.kgPatterns.map((p: KGPattern) => ({ ...p })),
      domainModel: parent.domainModel,
      // previousSteps 重新构造:只包含 Project 级资产
      previousSteps: {
        scope: parent.previousSteps['scope'],
        dfd: parent.previousSteps['dfd'],
        bpm: parent.previousSteps['bpm'],
        dict: projectDict,                  // 共享引用(Dict 是只读的)
        er: parent.previousSteps['er'],
        stateMachine: parent.previousSteps['stateMachine'],
        // 不复制前事件的 pspec/dt/ui
      },
      // 跨事件一致性检查用
      allDecisionTables: existingDecisionTables.slice(),  // 拷贝一份
      projectDict,                         // 暴露给 Agent
      currentEventId: eventId,
      // 重试状态:新事件全新开始
      lastErrors: undefined,
      userId: parent.userId,
      startTime: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 跑单步(带 retry + validator + DB 写入)
  // ============================================================
  async runStepWithValidation<T>(
    agentName: string,
    tableName: string,
    ctx: SAContext,
    saveToDb: (output: T) => Promise<void>,
    customValidator?: (output: T) => { passed: boolean; errors: ValidationError[] },
  ): Promise<T> {
    const agent = this.agents.get(agentName);
    if (!agent) throw new Error(`Agent ${agentName} not found`);

    const result = await runWithRetry<T>(
      tableName,
      ctx,
      this.db,
      { maxRetries: this.config.maxRetries, retryDelayMs: this.config.retryDelayMs },
      async () => await agent.generate(ctx),
      (output) => customValidator ? customValidator(output) : this.runDefaultValidator(agentName, output, ctx),
    );

    await saveToDb(result.output);
    return result.output;
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 默认 Validator 路由(根据 Agent 名选对应 Validator)
  // 判空保护:Validator 未注入时跳过校验(允许无校验模式运行)
  // ============================================================
  private runDefaultValidator(agentName: string, output: any, ctx: SAContext): { passed: boolean; errors: ValidationError[] } {
    try {
      switch (agentName) {
        case 'DFDAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.DFDValidator) return this.skipValidator('DFD');
          return new this.validators.DFDValidator(output).validate();
        }
        case 'BPMAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.BPMValidator) return this.skipValidator('BPM');
          const dfdProcesses = ctx.previousSteps['dfd']?.processes || [];
          return new this.validators.BPMValidator(output, dfdProcesses).validate();
        }
        case 'DictAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.DictValidator) return this.skipValidator('Dict');
          const dfd = ctx.previousSteps['dfd'] || { dataFlows: [], dataStores: [] };
          return new this.validators.DictValidator(output, dfd).validate();
        }
        case 'PSpecAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.LogicValidator) return this.skipValidator('PSpec');
          const dict = ctx.previousSteps['dict'];
          if (!dict) return { passed: true, errors: [] };
          return new this.validators.LogicValidator(output, dict).validate();
        }
        case 'DecisionTableAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.CrossEventConsistencyValidator) return this.skipValidator('DecisionTable');
          const allTables = ctx.allDecisionTables || [];
          return new this.validators.CrossEventConsistencyValidator(output, allTables).validate();
        }
        case 'ERAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.ERValidator) return this.skipValidator('ER');
          const dict = ctx.previousSteps['dict'];
          if (!dict) return { passed: true, errors: [] };
          return new this.validators.ERValidator(output, dict).validate();
        }
        case 'StateMachineAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.STDReachabilityValidator) return this.skipValidator('StateMachine');
          const dict = ctx.previousSteps['dict'];
          return new this.validators.STDReachabilityValidator(output, dict).validate();
        }
        case 'UIAgent': {
          if (!this.validators.UIValidator) return this.skipValidator('UI');
          const dict = ctx.previousSteps['dict'];
          const bpm = ctx.previousSteps['bpm'];
          if (!dict || !bpm) return { passed: true, errors: [] };
          return new this.validators.UIValidator(output, dict, bpm).validate();
        }
        default:
          return { passed: true, errors: [] };
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`[Validator] ${agentName} exception:`, e);
      return { passed: false, errors: [{ code: 'VALIDATOR_EXCEPTION', message: (e as Error).message, severity: 'ERROR' as const }] };
    }
  }

  private skipValidator(name: string) {
    console.warn(`[Validator] ${name}Validator 未注入,跳过校验`);
    return { passed: true, errors: [] };
  }

  // ============================================================
  // 解析 Context
  // ============================================================
  private async resolveContext(req: SARequest): Promise<SAContext> {
    const kgPatterns = await this.db.getProjectKGPatterns(req.projectId, 5);
    const industry = this.inferIndustry(req.requirementText);
    const domainModel = await this.db.getDomainModel(industry);

    return {
      tenantId: req.tenantId,
      projectId: req.projectId,
      requirementId: req.requirementId,
      requirementText: req.requirementText,
      eventId: req.eventId,
      eventDescription: req.eventDescription,
      assetLevel: req.assetLevel || 'PROJECT',
      kgPatterns,
      domainModel,
      previousSteps: {},
      userId: req.userId,
      startTime: Date.now(),
    };
  }

  private async loadExistingDecisionTables(ctx: SAContext): Promise<any[]> {
    return await this.db.getAllDecisionTablesInProject(ctx.projectId);
  }

  // 行业推断
  private inferIndustry(text: string): string {
    if (/MES|制造|工单|工序|报工|加工/.test(text)) return 'manufacturing';
    if (/电商|订单|购物|支付/.test(text)) return 'ecommerce';
    if (/眼镜|验光|处方/.test(text)) return 'optical';
    return 'general';
  }
}

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/orchestrator/Semaphore.ts ===
// Semaphore - 限制 LLM 并发数,避免 gateway 拒绝
// 60 个事件并发会让 LLM gateway OOM,所以限制到 N 个
export class Semaphore {
  private active = 0;
  private queue: Array<() => void> = [];

  constructor(public readonly max: number) {
    if (max < 1) throw new Error('Semaphore max must be >= 1');
  }

  /**
   * 在信号量保护下执行异步任务
   */
  async run<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    await this.acquire();
    try {
      return await fn();
    } finally {
      this.release();
    }
  }

  private async acquire(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.active < this.max) {
      this.active++;
      return;
    }
    // 进入等待队列(不持有任何引用,避免内存泄漏)
    await new Promise<void>(resolve => this.queue.push(resolve));
    this.active++;
  }

  private release(): void {
    this.active--;
    const next = this.queue.shift();
    if (next) next();
  }

  get stats() {
    return { active: this.active, queued: this.queue.length, max: this.max };
  }
}

=== END FILE:  ===

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/orchestrator/StepRouter.ts ===
// StepRouter - 真正的 6 问清单(检查 globalDict 决定事件复杂度)
// 取代之前的"只看 complexity 字段"的退化版本

import { SAOrchestrator } from './SAOrchestrator';
import { SAContext, ScopeOutput, DictOutput, ValidationError } from '../types';

export type EventComplexity = 'simple' | 'medium' | 'complex';
export type AssetLevel = 'PROJECT' | 'EVENT' | 'PROCESS';

export interface BusinessEvent {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  complexity: EventComplexity;
}

export interface SixQuestions {
  Q1_newEntity: boolean;        // 事件涉及 dict 里没有的新实体?
  Q2_newDataFlow: boolean;      // 事件涉及 dict 里没有的新数据流?
  Q3_stateTransition: boolean;  // 事件涉及状态扭转(状态机要扩展)?
  Q4_crossSystem: boolean;      // 事件跨外部系统(WMS/ERP/设备)?
  Q5_complexDecision: boolean;   // 事件含复杂判定(报废率/阈值/分级)?
  Q6_concurrency: boolean;      // 事件含并发/事务边界?
}

export interface TierDecision {
  assetLevel: AssetLevel;
  eventId: number;
  stepsToRun: string[];
  reason: string;
  sixQ: SixQuestions;
}

export interface EventAssets {
  eventId: number;
  assetLevel: AssetLevel;
  pspec?: any;
  decisionTable?: any;
  ui?: any;
}

/**
 * 六问清单 - 真正根据 globalDict + 事件描述判断
 * 不再只看 complexity 字段
 */
export function askSixQuestions(event: BusinessEvent, globalDict?: DictOutput): SixQuestions {
  const desc = `${event.name} ${event.description}`.toLowerCase();
  const entityNames = (globalDict?.dataStores || []).map(s => s.name.toLowerCase());
  const flowNames = (globalDict?.dataFlows || []).map(f => f.name.toLowerCase());
  const fieldNames = (globalDict?.elements || []).map(e => e.name.toLowerCase());

  // Q1: 事件涉及的实体是否在 dict 里
  // 启发式:事件描述里的关键词没匹配 dict 里任一实体名 → 新实体
  const allKnownNames = [...entityNames, ...flowNames, ...fieldNames];
  const Q1_newEntity = allKnownNames.length === 0
    ? true  // 没有 dict 时假设新实体
    : !allKnownNames.some(n => n && desc.includes(n));

  // Q2: 是否有新数据流(基于"数据"关键词)
  const dataKeywords = ['数据', 'data', 'flow', 'stream'];
  const hasDataKeyword = dataKeywords.some(k => desc.includes(k));
  const Q2_newDataFlow = hasDataKeyword && !flowNames.some(f => desc.includes(f));

  // Q3: 状态扭转(状态/报工/审批/驳回/关闭/完成/流转)
  const Q3_stateTransition = /状态|报工|审批|驳回|关闭|完成|流转|提交|取消|挂起/.test(desc);

  // Q4: 跨系统(WMS/ERP/MES/PLM/SAP/API/外部/第三方)
  const Q4_crossSystem = /erp|wms|plm|sap|api|外部|第三方|接口|推送|同步/.test(desc);

  // Q5: 复杂判定(报废/让步/规则/阈值/条件/分级/折扣/税率)
  const Q5_complexDecision = /报废|让步|判定|规则|阈值|条件|分级|折扣|税率|免审|强制/.test(desc);

  // Q6: 并发/事务(并发/事务/加锁/乐观/悲观/批量)
  const Q6_concurrency = /并发|事务|加锁|乐观|悲观|批量|同步|异步/.test(desc);

  return { Q1_newEntity, Q2_newDataFlow, Q3_stateTransition, Q4_crossSystem, Q5_complexDecision, Q6_concurrency };
}

/**
 * classifyEvent - 真正的 3-Tier 路由
 * 决策规则:
 *   - Q5 或 Q6 = PROCESS 级(需要 PSPEC + DecisionTable 深度推演)
 *   - Q1-Q4 任一 = EVENT 级 medium(扩展 Dict + 增量 PSPEC + UI)
 *   - 全部 false = EVENT 级 simple(只跑 UI,复用 Project 级资产)
 */
export function classifyEvent(event: BusinessEvent, globalDict?: DictOutput): TierDecision {
  const sixQ = askSixQuestions(event, globalDict);

  // PROCESS 级:复杂判定或并发(需要 PSPEC 事务边界 + 判定表阈值)
  if (sixQ.Q5_complexDecision || sixQ.Q6_concurrency) {
    return {
      assetLevel: 'PROCESS',
      eventId: event.id,
      stepsToRun: ['PSpecAgent', 'DecisionTableAgent', 'StateMachineAgent', 'UIAgent'],
      reason: `复杂事件 "${event.name}": PROCESS 级深度推演 (Q5=${sixQ.Q5_complexDecision} 或 Q6=${sixQ.Q6_concurrency})`,
      sixQ,
    };
  }

  // EVENT 级 medium:Q1-Q4 任一
  if (sixQ.Q1_newEntity || sixQ.Q2_newDataFlow || sixQ.Q3_stateTransition || sixQ.Q4_crossSystem) {
    const stepsToRun: string[] = ['UIAgent'];
    if (sixQ.Q3_stateTransition) stepsToRun.push('StateMachineAgent');
    if (sixQ.Q1_newEntity || sixQ.Q2_newDataFlow) stepsToRun.push('PSpecAgent');
    return {
      assetLevel: 'EVENT',
      eventId: event.id,
      stepsToRun,
      reason: `增量事件 "${event.name}": EVENT 级扩展 (Q1-Q4 至少一项)`,
      sixQ,
    };
  }

  // EVENT 级 simple:全部 false,复用 Project 级
  return {
    assetLevel: 'EVENT',
    eventId: event.id,
    stepsToRun: ['UIAgent'],
    reason: `简单事件 "${event.name}": 复用 Project 级资产,只生成 UI`,
    sixQ,
  };
}

/**
 * 跑 Step 1(总是先跑,作为整个流水线的入口)
 */
export async function runScopeStep(orchestrator: SAOrchestrator, ctx: SAContext): Promise<ScopeOutput> {
  return await orchestrator.runStepWithValidation<ScopeOutput>(
    'ScopeAgent',
    'sa_scope',
    ctx,
    async (output) => {
      const { id } = await orchestrator.db.saveScope(output, ctx);
      ctx.scopeId = id;
    },
    (output) => {
      const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
      if (output.eventCount === 0) {
        errors.push({ code: 'SCOPE_NO_EVENTS', message: 'Scope 没提取到任何业务事件', severity: 'ERROR' });
      }
      if (!output.systemBoundary?.inScope || output.systemBoundary.inScope.length === 0) {
        errors.push({ code: 'SCOPE_NO_INSCOPE', message: 'Scope 没定义 In Scope', severity: 'ERROR' });
      }
      return { passed: errors.length === 0, errors };
    }
  );
}

/**
 * 向后兼容:旧 decideSteps(只根据 complexity 字段)
 * 保留以防外部代码依赖,但新代码用 classifyEvent
 */
export interface StepDecision {
  runDFD: boolean;
  runBPM: boolean;
  runDict: boolean;
  runPSpec: boolean;
  runDecisionTable: boolean;
  runER: boolean;
  runStateMachine: boolean;
  runUI: boolean;
  reason: string;
}

export function decideSteps(complexity: EventComplexity, hasStateChange: boolean): StepDecision {
  switch (complexity) {
    case 'simple':
      return {
        runDFD: false, runBPM: false, runDict: false, runPSpec: false,
        runDecisionTable: false, runER: false, runStateMachine: false,
        runUI: true,
        reason: '简单事件:跳过 DFD/BPM/PSPEC/DT,只生成 UI',
      };
    case 'medium':
      return {
        runDFD: true, runBPM: true, runDict: true, runPSpec: true,
        runDecisionTable: false, runER: true,
        runStateMachine: hasStateChange, runUI: true,
        reason: '中等事件:跑 DFD/BPM/Dict/PSPEC/ER,按需跑 STD',
      };
    case 'complex':
      return {
        runDFD: true, runBPM: true, runDict: true, runPSpec: true,
        runDecisionTable: true, runER: true, runStateMachine: true, runUI: true,
        reason: '复杂事件:跑全部 9 步',
      };
  }
}

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/persistence/Logger.ts ===
// Logger 接口 - 简单实现,生产可替换
export interface Logger {
  info(msg: string): void;
  warn(msg: string): void;
  error(msg: string, err?: any): void;
  debug(msg: string): void;
}

export class ConsoleLogger implements Logger {
  private prefix: string;
  constructor(prefix = 'SA') {
    this.prefix = prefix;
  }
  info(msg: string): void { console.log(`[${this.prefix}/INFO]`, msg); }
  warn(msg: string): void { console.warn(`[${this.prefix}/WARN]`, msg); }
  error(msg: string, err?: any): void { console.error(`[${this.prefix}/ERROR]`, msg, err || ''); }
  debug(msg: string): void { if (process.env.DEBUG) console.log(`[${this.prefix}/DEBUG]`, msg); }
}

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/persistence/OfflineWriteQueue.ts ===
// OfflineWriteQueue - 断网时把 SA 写入暂存到本地文件
// 网络恢复时自动 flush 到 SQL Server
// ★ 设计: 单文件追加(JSONL),断电不丢

import * as fs from 'fs';
import * as path from 'path';

export interface QueuedWrite {
  sql: string;
  params: Record<string, any>;
  metadata?: {
    pipelineId?: string;
    pipelineStep?: number;
  };
  enqueuedAt: Date;
  attempts: number;
}

export interface Logger {
  warn(msg: string): void;
  error(msg: string, err?: any): void;
  info(msg: string): void;
}

export interface OfflineWriteQueueOptions {
  enabled?: boolean;
  dir?: string;
  maxQueueSize?: number;
  flushIntervalMs?: number;
  maxRetries?: number;
  logger?: Logger;
}

const DEFAULT_OPTIONS: Required<Omit<OfflineWriteQueueOptions, 'logger'>> = {
  enabled: true,
  dir: './sa-offline-queue',
  maxQueueSize: 10000,
  flushIntervalMs: 30000,
  maxRetries: 5,
};

export class OfflineWriteQueue {
  private options: Required<Omit<OfflineWriteQueueOptions, 'logger'>>;
  private logger: Logger;
  private isFlushing = false;
  private flushInterval: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;
  private queueFile: string;
  private replayHook?: (item: QueuedWrite) => Promise<{ success: boolean }>;

  constructor(opts: OfflineWriteQueueOptions = {}) {
    this.options = { ...DEFAULT_OPTIONS, ...opts };
    this.logger = opts.logger || this.defaultLogger();
    this.queueFile = path.join(this.options.dir, 'sa-writes.jsonl');

    if (this.options.enabled) {
      this.ensureDir();
      this.startPeriodicFlush();
    }
  }

  // ★ 注册 DB replay hook(由 SqlServerSADatabase 设置)
  setReplayHook(hook: (item: QueuedWrite) => Promise<{ success: boolean }>): void {
    this.replayHook = hook;
  }

  // 入队
  async enqueue(write: Omit<QueuedWrite, 'attempts'>): Promise<void> {
    if (!this.options.enabled) return;

    // 检查队列大小
    const currentSize = this.size();
    if (currentSize >= this.options.maxQueueSize) {
      this.logger.error(`[OfflineQueue] 队列满 (${currentSize}/${this.options.maxQueueSize}), 写入被丢弃`);
      return;
    }

    const item: QueuedWrite = { ...write, attempts: 0 };
    try {
      fs.appendFileSync(this.queueFile, JSON.stringify(item) + '\n');
      this.logger.warn(`[OfflineQueue] 入队 #${currentSize + 1}: pipeline=${item.metadata?.pipelineId} step=${item.metadata?.pipelineStep}`);
    } catch (err) {
      this.logger.error('[OfflineQueue] 写入失败', err);
    }
  }

  // 当前队列大小
  size(): number {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.queueFile)) return 0;
    const content = fs.readFileSync(this.queueFile, 'utf-8');
    return content.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim()).length;
  }

  // 启动周期 flush(每 30 秒试一次)
  private startPeriodicFlush(): void {
    if (this.flushInterval) return;
    this.flushInterval = setInterval(() => {
      this.flush().catch(() => {});
    }, this.options.flushIntervalMs);
  }

  // 立即 flush
  async flush(): Promise<{ replayed: number; failed: number }> {
    if (!this.options.enabled || this.isFlushing) return { replayed: 0, failed: 0 };
    if (!this.replayHook) return { replayed: 0, failed: 0 };
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.queueFile)) return { replayed: 0, failed: 0 };

    this.isFlushing = true;
    let replayed = 0;
    let failed = 0;

    try {
      // 把整个文件读入(简单实现,生产应该用游标)
      const content = fs.readFileSync(this.queueFile, 'utf-8');
      const lines = content.split('\n').filter(l => l.trim());

      if (lines.length === 0) {
        return { replayed: 0, failed: 0 };
      }

      this.logger.info(`[OfflineQueue] 尝试 flush ${lines.length} 条`);

      const survivingLines: string[] = [];
      for (const line of lines) {
        try {
          const item: QueuedWrite = JSON.parse(line);
          const result = await this.replayHook(item);
          if (result.success) {
            replayed++;
          } else {
            item.attempts++;
            if (item.attempts >= this.options.maxRetries) {
              failed++;
              this.logger.error(`[OfflineQueue] 放弃(超过 ${this.options.maxRetries} 次重试): ${item.sql.substring(0, 100)}`);
            } else {
              survivingLines.push(JSON.stringify(item));
            }
          }
        } catch (err) {
          failed++;
          this.logger.error('[OfflineQueue] 重放失败', err);
        }
      }

      // 写回剩余
      fs.writeFileSync(this.queueFile, survivingLines.join('\n') + (survivingLines.length > 0 ? '\n' : ''));

      this.logger.info(`[OfflineQueue] flush 完成: replayed=${replayed}, failed=${failed}, remaining=${survivingLines.length}`);
      return { replayed, failed };
    } finally {
      this.isFlushing = false;
    }
  }

  // 关闭(优雅)
  async close(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.flushInterval) {
      clearInterval(this.flushInterval);
      this.flushInterval = null;
    }
    await this.flush();
  }

  private ensureDir(): void {
    if (!fs.existsSync(this.options.dir)) {
      fs.mkdirSync(this.options.dir, { recursive: true });
    }
  }

  private defaultLogger(): Logger {
    return {
      info: (msg) => console.log(msg),
      warn: (msg) => console.warn(msg),
      error: (msg, err) => console.error(msg, err || ''),
    };
  }
}

// ISADatabase 实现 - in-memory mock(可直接替换为 SQL Server 实现)

import {
  ISADatabase, SAContext, ScopeOutput, DFDOutput, BPMOutput, DictOutput,
  PSpecOutput, DecisionTableOutput, EROutput, StateMachineOutput, UIOutput,
  ValidationLogRecord, KGPattern, DomainModelContext
} from '../types';

// =====================================================
// InMemorySADatabase - 内存实现(用于开发和测试)
// 生产环境替换为 SqlServerSADatabase
// 重要:每条记录都携带 assetLevel + eventId + projectId 三元组
// =====================================================
export class InMemorySADatabase implements ISADatabase {
  private scopes = new Map<number, any>();
  private dfds = new Map<number, any>();
  private bpms = new Map<number, any>();
  private dicts = new Map<number, any>();
  private pspecs = new Map<number, any>();
  private decisionTables = new Map<number, any>();
  private ers = new Map<number, any>();
  private stateMachines = new Map<number, any>();
  private uis = new Map<number, any>();
  private validationLogs: ValidationLogRecord[] = [];
  private nextId = 1;

  // ========================================================
  // 元数据辅助
  // ========================================================
  private meta(ctx: SAContext) {
    return {
      projectId: ctx.projectId,
      tenantId: ctx.tenantId,
      assetLevel: ctx.assetLevel || 'PROJECT',  // ★ 三级分层
      eventId: ctx.eventId,                       // ★ 单事件路由
      requirementId: ctx.requirementId,
      createdAt: new Date(),
    };
  }

  // ========================================================
  // Save 方法(每条记录都带 assetLevel + eventId)
  // ========================================================
  async saveScope(scope: ScopeOutput, ctx: SAContext): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.scopes.set(id, { id, ...scope, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    ctx.scopeId = id;
    return { id };
  }

  async saveDFD(dfd: DFDOutput, ctx: SAContext, scopeId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.scopes, scopeId, 'sa_scope');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.dfds.set(id, { id, ...dfd, scopeId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async saveBPM(bpm: BPMOutput, ctx: SAContext, dfdId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.dfds, dfdId, 'sa_dfd');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.bpms.set(id, { id, ...bpm, dfdId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async saveDict(dict: DictOutput, ctx: SAContext, dfdId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.dfds, dfdId, 'sa_dfd');
    this.fkCheck(this.bpms, bpmId, 'sa_business_process');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.dicts.set(id, { id, ...dict, dfdId, bpmId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async savePSpec(pspec: PSpecOutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.dicts, dictId, 'sa_data_dictionary');
    this.fkCheck(this.bpms, bpmId, 'sa_business_process');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.pspecs.set(id, { id, ...pspec, dictId, bpmId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async saveDecisionTable(dt: DecisionTableOutput, ctx: SAContext, pspecId: number, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.pspecs, pspecId, 'sa_pspec');
    this.fkCheck(this.dicts, dictId, 'sa_data_dictionary');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.decisionTables.set(id, { id, ...dt, pspecId, dictId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async saveER(er: EROutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.dicts, dictId, 'sa_data_dictionary');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.ers.set(id, { id, ...er, dictId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async saveStateMachine(sm: StateMachineOutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.dicts, dictId, 'sa_data_dictionary');
    this.fkCheck(this.bpms, bpmId, 'sa_business_process');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.stateMachines.set(id, { id, ...sm, dictId, bpmId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  async saveUI(ui: UIOutput, ctx: SAContext, bpmId: number, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    this.fkCheck(this.bpms, bpmId, 'sa_business_process');
    this.fkCheck(this.dicts, dictId, 'sa_data_dictionary');
    const id = this.nextId++;
    const m = this.meta(ctx);
    this.uis.set(id, { id, ...ui, bpmId, dictId, ...m, isCurrent: true });
    return { id };
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 强外键检查(防止凭空生成)
  // ========================================================
  private fkCheck(map: Map<number, any>, id: number, targetTable: string) {
    if (!map.has(id)) {
      throw new Error(
        `强外键违规: ${targetTable}.id=${id} 不存在;必须先创建上游记录`
      );
    }
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 校验日志
  // ========================================================
  async logValidation(record: ValidationLogRecord): Promise<void> {
    this.validationLogs.push(record);
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 读取方法
  // ========================================================
  async getProjectKGPatterns(projectId: number, limit = 5): Promise<KGPattern[]> {
    return [];
  }

  async getDomainModel(industry: string): Promise<DomainModelContext> {
    return { industry, standardFields: [], standardEntities: [], standardProcesses: [] };
  }

  async getAllDecisionTablesInProject(projectId: number): Promise<any[]> {
    return Array.from(this.decisionTables.values())
      .filter(dt => dt.projectId === projectId);
  }

  // ★ 新增:按 eventId 查 SA 资产(Event 级资产隔离)
  async getAssetsByEvent(eventId: number): Promise<{
    scopes: any[]; dfds: any[]; bpms: any[]; dicts: any[];
    pspecs: any[]; decisionTables: any[]; ers: any[];
    stateMachines: any[]; uis: any[];
  }> {
    return {
      scopes: this.filterByEvent(this.scopes, eventId),
      dfds: this.filterByEvent(this.dfds, eventId),
      bpms: this.filterByEvent(this.bpms, eventId),
      dicts: this.filterByEvent(this.dicts, eventId),
      pspecs: this.filterByEvent(this.pspecs, eventId),
      decisionTables: this.filterByEvent(this.decisionTables, eventId),
      ers: this.filterByEvent(this.ers, eventId),
      stateMachines: this.filterByEvent(this.stateMachines, eventId),
      uis: this.filterByEvent(this.uis, eventId),
    };
  }

  private filterByEvent(map: Map<number, any>, eventId: number): any[] {
    return Array.from(map.values()).filter(r => r.eventId === eventId);
  }

  // ★ 新增:按 assetLevel 查(Project 级 vs Event 级 vs Process 级)
  async getAssetsByLevel(projectId: number, assetLevel: string): Promise<any[]> {
    const all = [
      ...Array.from(this.scopes.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.dfds.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.bpms.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.dicts.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.pspecs.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.decisionTables.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.ers.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.stateMachines.values()),
      ...Array.from(this.uis.values()),
    ];
    return all.filter(r => r.projectId === projectId && r.assetLevel === assetLevel);
  }

  // 测试辅助
  getValidationLogs(): ValidationLogRecord[] {
    return this.validationLogs;
  }

  getStats() {
    return {
      scopes: this.scopes.size,
      dfds: this.dfds.size,
      bpms: this.bpms.size,
      dicts: this.dicts.size,
      pspecs: this.pspecs.size,
      decisionTables: this.decisionTables.size,
      ers: this.ers.size,
      stateMachines: this.stateMachines.size,
      uis: this.uis.size,
      validationLogs: this.validationLogs.length,
    };
  }
}

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/persistence/SqlServerSADatabase.ts ===
// SqlServerSADatabase - 生产级 SA 数据库实现
// 基于 mssql 库,使用 sa_schema.sql 创建的 10 张表
// ★ 关键特性:
// 1. 强外键检查(写入前先查父表存在)
// 2. 离线写入队列(本地文件落盘,断网不丢数据)
// 3. SCD Type 2 版本管理(改记录 = 新 version + 旧 is_current=0)
// 4. 增量查询(查已完成事件,跳过重跑)
// 5. Pipeline 元数据(pipeline_id/pipeline_step/step_name)

import {
  ISADatabase, SAContext, ScopeOutput, DFDOutput, BPMOutput, DictOutput,
  PSpecOutput, DecisionTableOutput, EROutput, StateMachineOutput, UIOutput,
  ValidationLogRecord, KGPattern, DomainModelContext,
} from '../types';
import { OfflineWriteQueue } from './OfflineWriteQueue';
import { Logger } from './Logger';

// =====================================================
// SQL Server 数据库连接池类型(microsoft/mssql)
// =====================================================
export interface SqlServerConfig {
  server: string;
  port?: number;
  database: string;
  user: string;
  password: string;
  pool?: {
    max?: number;
    min?: number;
    idleTimeoutMillis?: number;
  };
  requestTimeout?: number;
  connectionTimeout?: number;
}

// 简化的 mssql 客户端接口(避免硬依赖)
// 实际生产用 mssql.ConnectionPool
export interface MssqlPool {
  request(): MssqlRequest;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export interface MssqlRequest {
  input(name: string, value: any): MssqlRequest;
  query(sql: string): Promise<MssqlQueryResult>;
  query(sql: string, callback: (err: any, result: MssulRecordSet) => void): void;
}

export interface MssqlQueryResult {
  recordset: any[];
  rowsAffected: number[];
}

export interface MssulRecordSet extends MssqlQueryResult {}

// =====================================================
// SqlServerSADatabase
// =====================================================
export class SqlServerSADatabase implements ISADatabase {
  private connected = false;
  private offlineQueue: OfflineWriteQueue;

  constructor(
    private poolFactory: () => Promise<MssqlPool>,
    private options: {
      enableOfflineQueue?: boolean;
      queueDir?: string;
      logger?: Logger;
    } = {}
  ) {
    this.offlineQueue = new OfflineWriteQueue({
      enabled: options.enableOfflineQueue ?? true,
      dir: options.queueDir || './sa-offline-queue',
      logger: options.logger,
    });
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 通用 SQL 助手:执行 + 离线队列回退
  // ========================================================
  private async execute(
    sql: string,
    params: Record<string, any>,
    metadata?: { pipelineId?: string; pipelineStep?: number }
  ): Promise<MssqlQueryResult> {
    try {
      const pool = await this.poolFactory();
      const req = pool.request();
      for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) {
        req.input(k, v);
      }
      return await req.query(sql);
    } catch (err) {
      // DB 不可用 - 进离线队列
      if (this.options.enableOfflineQueue !== false) {
        await this.offlineQueue.enqueue({ sql, params, metadata, enqueuedAt: new Date() });
        // 返回空结果(调用方要处理)
        return { recordset: [], rowsAffected: [0] };
      }
      throw err;
    }
  }

  // ========================================================
  // Save 方法 - 10 张表全部带 pipeline 元数据
  // ========================================================

  async saveScope(scope: ScopeOutput, ctx: SAContext): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_scope (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        system_boundary, external_entities, business_events, event_count,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @system_boundary, @external_entities, @business_events, @event_count,
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        system_boundary: JSON.stringify(scope.systemBoundary),
        external_entities: JSON.stringify(scope.externalEntities),
        business_events: JSON.stringify(scope.businessEvents),
        event_count: scope.eventCount,
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveDFD(dfd: DFDOutput, ctx: SAContext, scopeId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_scope', scopeId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_dfd (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        scope_id, context_diagram, dfd_levels, processes, data_flows, data_stores,
        balance_check, conservation_check,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @scope_id, @context_diagram, @dfd_levels, @processes, @data_flows, @data_stores,
        NULL, NULL,  -- Validator 写回
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        scope_id: scopeId,
        context_diagram: JSON.stringify(dfd.contextDiagram),
        dfd_levels: JSON.stringify(dfd.dfdLevels),
        processes: JSON.stringify(dfd.processes),
        data_flows: JSON.stringify(dfd.dataFlows),
        data_stores: JSON.stringify(dfd.dataStores),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveBPM(bpm: BPMOutput, ctx: SAContext, dfdId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_dfd', dfdId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_business_process (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dfd_id, swim_lanes, activity_nodes, edges, exception_paths, dfd_process_mappings,
        mapping_validation,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @dfd_id, @swim_lanes, @activity_nodes, @edges, @exception_paths, @dfd_process_mappings,
        NULL,  -- Validator 写回
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        dfd_id: dfdId,
        swim_lanes: JSON.stringify(bpm.swimLanes),
        activity_nodes: JSON.stringify(bpm.activityNodes),
        edges: JSON.stringify(bpm.edges),
        exception_paths: JSON.stringify(bpm.exceptionPaths || []),
        dfd_process_mappings: JSON.stringify(bpm.dfdProcessMappings),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveDict(dict: DictOutput, ctx: SAContext, dfdId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_dfd', dfdId, ctx);
    await this.fkCheck('sa_business_process', bpmId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_data_dictionary (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dfd_id, bpm_id, elements, data_structures, data_flows, data_stores,
        type_check, length_check, constraint_check, fk_reference_check, has_tenant_id, has_audit_fields,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @dfd_id, @bpm_id, @elements, @data_structures, @data_flows, @data_stores,
        NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL,  -- Validator 写回
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        dfd_id: dfdId,
        bpm_id: bpmId,
        elements: JSON.stringify(dict.elements),
        data_structures: JSON.stringify(dict.dataStores),
        data_flows: JSON.stringify(dict.dataFlows),
        data_stores: JSON.stringify(dict.dataStores),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async savePSpec(pspec: PSpecOutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_data_dictionary', dictId, ctx);
    await this.fkCheck('sa_business_process', bpmId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_pspec (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dict_id, bpm_id, process_specs,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @dict_id, @bpm_id, @process_specs,
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        dict_id: dictId,
        bpm_id: bpmId,
        process_specs: JSON.stringify(pspec.processSpecs),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveDecisionTable(dt: DecisionTableOutput, ctx: SAContext, pspecId: number, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_pspec', pspecId, ctx);
    await this.fkCheck('sa_data_dictionary', dictId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_decision_table (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        pspec_id, dict_id, tables,
        cross_event_consistency, condition_whitelist_check, completeness_check, has_default_rule,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @pspec_id, @dict_id, @tables,
        NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL,  -- Validator 写回
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        pspec_id: pspecId,
        dict_id: dictId,
        tables: JSON.stringify(dt.tables),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveER(er: EROutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_data_dictionary', dictId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_er (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dict_id, entities, relationships,
        third_normal_form, fk_in_dict, no_calculated_columns,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @dict_id, @entities, @relationships,
        NULL, NULL, NULL,  -- Validator 写回
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        dict_id: dictId,
        entities: JSON.stringify(er.entities),
        relationships: JSON.stringify(er.relationships),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveStateMachine(sm: StateMachineOutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_data_dictionary', dictId, ctx);
    await this.fkCheck('sa_business_process', bpmId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_state_machine (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dict_id, bpm_id, state_machines,
        states_in_dict, bpm_state_change_match, reachability_check, dead_end_check,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @dict_id, @bpm_id, @state_machines,
        NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL,  -- 这些是 Validator 写回,LLM 阶段不填
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        dict_id: dictId,
        bpm_id: bpmId,
        state_machines: JSON.stringify(sm.stateMachines),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  async saveUI(ui: UIOutput, ctx: SAContext, bpmId: number, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }> {
    await this.fkCheck('sa_business_process', bpmId, ctx);
    await this.fkCheck('sa_data_dictionary', dictId, ctx);
    const m = this.metadata(ctx);
    const result = await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_ui (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        bpm_id, dict_id, screens,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at
      ) OUTPUT INSERTED.id
      VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @asset_level, @event_id,
        @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step, @step_name,
        @bpm_id, @dict_id, @screens,
        'PENDING', 0,
        @created_by, GETDATE(), @created_by, GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        ...m,
        bpm_id: bpmId,
        dict_id: dictId,
        screens: JSON.stringify(ui.screens),
      }
    );
    return { id: result.recordset[0].id };
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 校验日志(独立表,无强外键)
  // ========================================================
  async logValidation(record: ValidationLogRecord): Promise<void> {
    await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_validation_log (
        tenant_id, project_id, pipeline_id, pipeline_step,
        sa_table_name, sa_record_id, validator_name,
        retry_count, previous_errors, is_converged,
        validation_status, errors, duration_ms,
        created_at
      ) VALUES (
        @tenant_id, @project_id, @pipeline_id, @pipeline_step,
        @sa_table_name, @sa_record_id, @validator_name,
        @retry_count, @previous_errors, @is_converged,
        @validation_status, @errors, @duration_ms,
        GETDATE()
      )`,
      {
        tenant_id: record.tenantId,
        project_id: record.projectId,
        pipeline_id: (record as any).pipelineId || null,
        pipeline_step: (record as any).pipelineStep || null,
        sa_table_name: record.saTableName,
        sa_record_id: record.saRecordId || 0,
        validator_name: record.validatorName,
        retry_count: record.retryCount,
        previous_errors: JSON.stringify(record.previousErrors),
        is_converged: record.isConverged ? 1 : 0,
        validation_status: record.validationStatus,
        errors: JSON.stringify(record.errors),
        duration_ms: record.durationMs,
      }
    );
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 读方法
  // ========================================================
  async getProjectKGPatterns(projectId: number, limit = 5): Promise<KGPattern[]> {
    const result = await this.execute(
      `SELECT TOP (@limit) id, pattern_type, industry, pattern_content, score, pattern_tags
       FROM kg_pattern
       WHERE is_active = 1 AND deprecated_at IS NULL AND score >= 0.6
       ORDER BY score DESC, usage_count DESC`,
      { limit }
    );
    return result.recordset.map((r: any) => ({
      id: r.id,
      type: r.pattern_type,
      industry: r.industry,
      content: JSON.parse(r.pattern_content),
      score: r.score,
      tags: JSON.parse(r.pattern_tags || '[]'),
    }));
  }

  async getDomainModel(industry: string): Promise<DomainModelContext> {
    const result = await this.execute(
      `SELECT TOP 1 industry, standard_fields, standard_entities, standard_processes
       FROM domain_model
       WHERE industry = @industry AND is_active = 1`,
      { industry }
    );
    if (result.recordset.length === 0) {
      return { industry, standardFields: [], standardEntities: [], standardProcesses: [] };
    }
    const r = result.recordset[0];
    return {
      industry: r.industry,
      standardFields: JSON.parse(r.standard_fields || '[]'),
      standardEntities: JSON.parse(r.standard_entities || '[]'),
      standardProcesses: JSON.parse(r.standard_processes || '[]'),
    };
  }

  async getAllDecisionTablesInProject(projectId: number): Promise<any[]> {
    const result = await this.execute(
      `SELECT id, tables, cross_event_consistency
       FROM sa_decision_table
       WHERE project_id = @projectId AND is_current = 1 AND is_deleted = 0`,
      { projectId }
    );
    return result.recordset.map((r: any) => ({
      id: r.id,
      tables: JSON.parse(r.tables),
      cross_event_consistency: r.cross_event_consistency === 1,
    }));
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 增量查询: 哪些事件已经跑完所有 9 步?
  // ========================================================
  async getCompletedEventIds(projectId: number, tenantId: string): Promise<number[]> {
    const result = await this.execute(
      `SELECT event_id
       FROM sa_scope
       WHERE project_id = @projectId
         AND tenant_id = @tenantId
         AND is_current = 1
         AND event_id IS NOT NULL
         AND validation_status = 'PASS'
       GROUP BY event_id
       HAVING COUNT(DISTINCT pipeline_step) = 9`,  // 9 步全跑过
      { projectId, tenantId }
    );
    return result.recordset.map((r: any) => r.event_id);
  }

  // 查某事件的当前活跃资产(用于二次开发只改相关)
  async getCurrentAssetsByEvent(
    projectId: number, tenantId: string, eventId: number
  ): Promise<{
    scope: any | null;
    dfd: any | null;
    bpm: any | null;
    dict: any | null;
    pspec: any | null;
    decisionTable: any | null;
    er: any | null;
    stateMachine: any | null;
    ui: any | null;
  }> {
    // 并发查 9 张表
    const [scope, dfd, bpm, dict, pspec, dt, er, sm, ui] = await Promise.all([
      this.findLatest('sa_scope', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_dfd', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_business_process', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_data_dictionary', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_pspec', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_decision_table', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_er', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_state_machine', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
      this.findLatest('sa_ui', projectId, tenantId, eventId),
    ]);
    return { scope, dfd, bpm, dict, pspec, decisionTable: dt, er, stateMachine: sm, ui };
  }

  private async findLatest(table: string, projectId: number, tenantId: string, eventId: number): Promise<any | null> {
    const result = await this.execute(
      `SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ${table}
       WHERE project_id = @projectId
         AND tenant_id = @tenantId
         AND event_id = @eventId
         AND is_current = 1 AND is_deleted = 0
       ORDER BY version DESC`,
      { projectId, tenantId, eventId }
    );
    return result.recordset[0] || null;
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 人改 dict(更新现有记录,SCD Type 2)
  // ========================================================
  async updateDict(dictId: number, elements: any, userId: string): Promise<void> {
    // SCD Type 2: 把旧版本 is_current=0,新增 v+1 版本
    await this.execute(
      `UPDATE sa_data_dictionary
       SET is_current = 0, valid_to = GETDATE()
       WHERE id = @dictId AND is_current = 1`,
      { dictId }
    );
    await this.execute(
      `INSERT INTO sa_data_dictionary (
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dfd_id, bpm_id, elements,
        validation_status, human_confirmed,
        created_by, created_at, updated_by, updated_at,
        version, is_current, valid_from
      )
      SELECT
        tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, event_id,
        pipeline_id, pipeline_step, step_name,
        dfd_id, bpm_id, @elements,
        'PASS', 1,
        @userId, GETDATE(), @userId, GETDATE(),
        version + 1, 1, GETDATE()
      FROM sa_data_dictionary
      WHERE id = @dictId`,
      { dictId, elements: JSON.stringify(elements), userId }
    );
  }

  // ========================================================
  // 元数据助手 + 强外键检查
  // ========================================================
  private metadata(ctx: SAContext): Record<string, any> {
    return {
      tenant_id: ctx.tenantId,
      project_id: ctx.projectId,
      asset_level: ctx.assetLevel || 'PROJECT',
      event_id: ctx.currentEventId ?? ctx.eventId ?? null,
      pipeline_id: (ctx as any).pipelineId ?? null,
      pipeline_step: (ctx as any).pipelineStep ?? null,
      step_name: (ctx as any).stepName ?? null,
      created_by: ctx.userId,
    };
  }

  private async fkCheck(table: string, id: number, ctx: SAContext): Promise<void> {
    if (id == null || id === 0) {
      throw new Error(`强外键违规: ${table} ID 为空 (project=${ctx.projectId}, tenant=${ctx.tenantId})`);
    }
    // 通过临时 SELECT 验证存在(简化 - 实际可加缓存)
    const idCol = table === 'sa_business_process' ? 'id' : 'id';
    const result = await this.execute(
      `SELECT TOP 1 id FROM ${table} WHERE id = @id AND is_deleted = 0`,
      { id }
    );
    if (result.recordset.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(`强外键违规: ${table}(id=${id}) 不存在或已删除`);
    }
  }

  // 优雅关闭
  async close(): Promise<void> {
    await this.offlineQueue.flush();
    // pool.close() 由调用方管理
  }
}

// Validator 类型定义(SDK 与 validators 包共享)

export type ValidationSeverity = 'ERROR' | 'WARNING';

export interface ValidationError {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  severity: ValidationSeverity;
  field?: string;
  suggestion?: string;
}

export interface ValidationResult {
  passed: boolean;
  errors: ValidationError[];
}

=== FILE: ./sa-sdk/src/types.ts ===
// SA SDK - 共享类型定义

import { ValidationError } from './types-validator';
export { Semaphore } from './orchestrator/Semaphore';

// =====================================================
// 客户需求
// =====================================================
export interface SARequest {
  tenantId: string;
  projectId: number;
  requirementId: number;
  requirementText: string;          // 客户原始需求
  eventId?: number;                  // 单事件模式(可选)
  eventDescription?: string;         // 单事件描述(可选)
  assetLevel?: 'PROJECT' | 'EVENT' | 'PROCESS';
  userId: string;                    // 触发人
}

// =====================================================
// SA 步骤产出(对应 9 张表)
// =====================================================
export interface ScopeOutput {
  systemBoundary: { inScope: string[]; outOfScope: string[] };
  externalEntities: Array<{ name: string; type: string; description: string }>;
  businessEvents: Array<{
    id: number;
    name: string;
    description: string;
    complexity: 'simple' | 'medium' | 'complex';
  }>;
  eventCount: number;
}

export interface DFDOutput {
  contextDiagram: any;
  dfdLevels: any;
  processes: Array<{ id: string; name: string; inputFlows: string[]; outputFlows: string[]; parentId?: string }>;
  dataFlows: Array<{ name: string }>;
  dataStores: Array<{ name: string }>;
}

export interface BPMOutput {
  swimLanes: any[];
  activityNodes: any[];
  edges: any[];
  exceptionPaths: any[];
  dfdProcessMappings: Record<string, string>;
}

export interface DictOutput {
  elements: Array<{
    name: string;
    type: string;
    isFK?: boolean;
    refEntity?: string;
    isRequired?: boolean;
  }>;
  dataFlows: Array<{ name: string; fields: any[] }>;
  dataStores: Array<{ name: string; fields: any[] }>;
}

export interface PSpecOutput {
  processSpecs: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    input: string[];
    output: string[];
    validation?: string;
    algorithm?: string;
  }>;
}

export interface DecisionTableOutput {
  tables: Array<{
    id: string;
    conditions: Array<{ name: string; operator: string; value: any }>;
    actions: Array<{ name: string }>;
    rules: Array<{ conditionMask: boolean[]; actionIndex: number }>;
  }>;
}

export interface EROutput {
  entities: Array<{ name: string; columns: any[] }>;
  relationships: any[];
}

export interface StateMachineOutput {
  stateMachines: Array<{
    entity: string;
    states: string[];
    transitions: Array<{ from: string; to: string; trigger: string }>;
  }>;
}

export interface UIOutput {
  screens: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    dataFlow: string;
    bpmNodeId: string;
    fields: Array<{ name: string; type: string; required: boolean; controlType: string }>;
  }>;
}

// =====================================================
// SA 总产出
// =====================================================
export interface SAOutput {
  projectId: number;
  tenantId: string;
  scope: ScopeOutput;
  dfd?: DFDOutput;
  bpm?: BPMOutput;
  dict?: DictOutput;
  pspec?: PSpecOutput;                       // 兼容旧 API(第一个事件的 PSpec)
  decisionTable?: DecisionTableOutput;        // 兼容旧 API(第一个事件的 DT)
  er?: EROutput;
  stateMachine?: StateMachineOutput;
  ui?: UIOutput;                              // 兼容旧 API(第一个事件的 UI)
  eventAssets?: Map<number, EventAssets>;     // ★ 新字段:每事件独立产出
  eventErrors?: Map<number, string>;          // ★ 新字段:失败的事件
  metadata: {
    totalDuration: number;
    totalRetries: number;
    validationStats: any[];
    eventCount?: number;
    eventSuccessCount?: number;
    eventErrorCount?: number;
    concurrency?: { active: number; queued: number; max: number };
  };
}

// =====================================================
// 单事件产出(新结构,每事件独立)
// =====================================================
export interface EventAssets {
  eventId: number;
  assetLevel: 'PROJECT' | 'EVENT' | 'PROCESS';
  pspec?: PSpecOutput;
  decisionTable?: DecisionTableOutput;
  stateMachine?: StateMachineOutput;
  ui?: UIOutput;
}

// =====================================================
// 业务事件(供 StepRouter + SAOrchestrator 共用)
// =====================================================
export interface BusinessEvent {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  complexity: 'simple' | 'medium' | 'complex';
}

// =====================================================
// SAContext - 补齐字段
// =====================================================

// (在现有 SAContext 定义下方补充中间 ID / projectDict / currentEventId / allDecisionTables 字段)

// =====================================================
// SA 上下文(贯穿整个流水线)
// =====================================================
export interface SAContext {
  tenantId: string;
  projectId: number;
  requirementId: number;
  requirementText: string;
  eventId?: number;
  eventDescription?: string;
  assetLevel: 'PROJECT' | 'EVENT' | 'PROCESS';

  // 上下文注入
  kgPatterns: KGPattern[];                // 知识图谱注入
  domainModel: DomainModelContext;        // 领域模型注入
  previousSteps: Record<string, any>;     // 上一步产出(供下一步使用)

  // 重试闭环
  lastErrors?: string[];

  // ★ 中间 ID(供 DB 写入强外键链用)
  scopeId?: number;
  dfdId?: number;
  bpmId?: number;
  dictId?: number;
  pspecId?: number;
  decisionTableId?: number;
  erId?: number;
  stateMachineId?: number;
  uiId?: number;

  // ★ 3-Tier: Project 级全局字典(Event 级复用)
  projectDict?: DictOutput;
  currentEventId?: number;

  // ★ 跨事件一致性检查
  allDecisionTables?: any[];

  // 元数据
  userId: string;
  startTime: number;
}

export interface KGPattern {
  id: string;
  type: 'field_naming' | 'decision_rule' | 'state_machine' | 'process_pattern';
  content: any;
  score: number;
  tags: string[];
}

export interface DomainModelContext {
  industry: string;
  standardFields: Array<{ name: string; type: string; description: string }>;
  standardEntities: Array<{ name: string; description: string; commonFields: string[] }>;
  standardProcesses: Array<{ id: string; name: string; description: string }>;
}

// =====================================================
// LLM 客户端接口(任何 LLM 都能接入)
// =====================================================
export interface ILLMClient {
  generate(params: {
    systemPrompt: string;
    context: Record<string, any>;
    lastErrors?: string[];
    temperature?: number;
  }): Promise<any>;
}

// =====================================================
// 数据库接口
// =====================================================
export interface ISADatabase {
  // 写入
  saveScope(scope: ScopeOutput, ctx: SAContext): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveDFD(dfd: DFDOutput, ctx: SAContext, scopeId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveBPM(bpm: BPMOutput, ctx: SAContext, dfdId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveDict(dict: DictOutput, ctx: SAContext, dfdId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  savePSpec(pspec: PSpecOutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveDecisionTable(dt: DecisionTableOutput, ctx: SAContext, pspecId: number, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveER(er: EROutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveStateMachine(sm: StateMachineOutput, ctx: SAContext, dictId: number, bpmId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;
  saveUI(ui: UIOutput, ctx: SAContext, bpmId: number, dictId: number): Promise<{ id: number }>;

  // 校验日志
  logValidation(record: ValidationLogRecord): Promise<void>;

  // 读取
  getProjectKGPatterns(projectId: number, limit?: number): Promise<KGPattern[]>;
  getDomainModel(industry: string): Promise<DomainModelContext>;
  getAllDecisionTablesInProject(projectId: number): Promise<any[]>;
}

export interface ValidationLogRecord {
  tenantId: string;
  projectId: number;
  saTableName: string;
  saRecordId?: number;
  validatorName: string;
  retryCount: number;
  previousErrors: string[] | null;
  isConverged: boolean;
  validationStatus: 'PASS' | 'FAIL';
  errors: ValidationError[];
  durationMs: number;
}

// =====================================================
// SA 配置
// =====================================================
export interface SAConfig {
  maxRetries: number;
  retryDelayMs: number;
  enableDKEE: boolean;
  enableCrossEventCheck: boolean;
  logLevel: 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';
}

export const DEFAULT_SA_CONFIG: SAConfig = {
  maxRetries: 5,
  retryDelayMs: 1000,
  enableDKEE: true,
  enableCrossEventCheck: true,
  logLevel: 'info',
};

// =====================================================
// SSE 进度事件(逐步推送到前端)
// =====================================================
export type SAProgressEvent =
  | { type: 'started'; projectId: number; assetLevel: AssetLevel; pipelineId: string }
  | { type: 'step_start'; step: string }
  | { type: 'step_done'; step: string; eventCount?: number; elementCount?: number; screenCount?: number; durationMs?: number }
  | { type: 'routing_decided'; step: string; message: string; decision?: any }
  | { type: 'event_step_start'; step: string; eventId: number }
  | { type: 'event_step_done'; step: string; eventId: number }
  | { type: 'event_failed'; eventId: number; error: string }
  | { type: 'retry'; step: string; attempt: number; errors: ValidationError[] }
  | { type: 'dkee_start' }
  | { type: 'dkee_done' }
  | { type: 'all_done'; totalDuration: number }
  | { type: 'failed'; step: string; errors: ValidationError[] };

export type AssetLevel = 'PROJECT' | 'EVENT' | 'PROCESS';

// =====================================================
// Pipeline 元数据(每个 SA 步骤写入 DB 时必须带)
// =====================================================
export interface PipelineMetadata {
  pipelineId: string;     // runSA() 的 UUID
  pipelineStep: number;   // 1-9
  stepName: string;       // 'Scope' / 'DFD' / 'BPM' / 'Dict' / 'PSpec' / 'DecisionTable' / 'ER' / 'StateMachine' / 'UI'
}

// 重新导出 Validator 类型供 SDK 使用
export { ValidationError } from './types-validator';
