=== FILE: ./sa-service/src/server.ts ===
// sa-service/src/server.ts - 重写版
// ★ 修复(对比上一版):
// 1. Validators 全部真实注入(不再 null)
// 2. 返回完整 9 步结果(scope/dfd/bpm/dict/pspec/dt/er/std/ui)
// 3. SSE 内存泄漏修复:clearInterval + 错误处理 + 自动注销
// 4. 异步 runSA + 任务追踪,避免长连接阻塞 Express
// 5. LLM gateway 错误处理 + 超时

import express, { Request, Response } from 'express';
import cors from 'cors';
import { v4 as uuidv4 } from 'uuid';
import { SAOrchestrator, ValidatorBundle, DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENCY } from './orchestrator/SAOrchestrator';
import { InMemorySADatabase } from './orchestrator/SADatabase';
import {
  ILLMClient, SARequest, SAOutput, SAConfig, DEFAULT_SA_CONFIG,
  SAProgressEvent,
} from './orchestrator/orchestrator-types';
import { Semaphore } from './orchestrator/Semaphore';

// =====================================================
// 1. LLM 客户端(支持超时 + 重试 + JSON 解析)
// =====================================================
class HttpLlmClient implements ILLMClient {
  constructor(
    private gatewayUrl: string,
    private apiKey?: string,
    private tenantId?: string,
    private authHeader?: string,
    private providerCode: string = 'deepseek',
    private defaultTemperature: number = 0.3,
    private defaultMaxTokens: number = 4096,
    private timeoutMs: number = 60_000,
  ) {}

  async generate(params: {
    systemPrompt: string;
    context: Record<string, any>;
    lastErrors?: string[];
    temperature?: number;
  }): Promise<any> {
    const enrichedContext = { ...params.context };
    if (params.lastErrors && params.lastErrors.length > 0) {
      enrichedContext._lastErrors = params.lastErrors;
    }

    const body = {
      providerCode: this.providerCode,
      systemPrompt: params.systemPrompt,
      messages: [{ role: 'user' as const, content: JSON.stringify(enrichedContext) }],
      temperature: params.temperature ?? this.defaultTemperature,
      maxTokens: this.defaultMaxTokens,
    };

    const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' };
    if (this.apiKey) headers['Authorization'] = `Bearer ${this.apiKey}`;
    if (this.tenantId) {
      headers['X-Tenant-Id'] = this.tenantId === 'default' ? '1' : this.tenantId;
    }
    if (this.authHeader) {
      headers['Authorization'] = this.authHeader;
    }

    // 用 AbortController 实现超时
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), this.timeoutMs);

    try {
      const response = await fetch(this.gatewayUrl, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers,
        body: JSON.stringify(body),
        signal: controller.signal,
      });
      if (!response.ok) {
        throw new Error(`LLM Gateway 返回 ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
      }

      const raw: any = await response.json();
      const payload = raw?.data ?? raw;
      if (payload?.isSuccess === false || payload?.IsSuccess === false) {
        throw new Error(String(payload?.error ?? payload?.Error ?? raw?.msg ?? 'LLM Gateway 返回失败'));
      }

      const content = payload?.content ?? payload?.Content ?? raw?.content ?? raw?.choices?.[0]?.message?.content ?? raw?.result ?? payload;

      if (typeof content === 'string') {
        const jsonMatch = content.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```/) || [null, content];
        try { return JSON.parse(jsonMatch[1]!.trim()); } catch { return content; }
      }
      return content;
    } catch (err) {
      if ((err as Error).name === 'AbortError') {
        throw new Error(`LLM 调用超时 (${this.timeoutMs}ms)`);
      }
      throw err;
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timeoutId);
    }
  }
}

// =====================================================
// 2. SSE Manager(防内存泄漏)
// =====================================================
interface SSEClient {
  id: string;
  projectId: number;
  res: Response;
  heartbeat: NodeJS.Timeout;
  createdAt: number;
}

class SSEManager {
  // ★ 用 Map 而不是数组 - O(1) 删除
  private clients = new Map<string, SSEClient>();

  // 单客户端上限 - 防止单个客户端占用过多
  private static readonly MAX_PER_PROJECT = 50;

  create(client: Omit<SSEClient, 'heartbeat' | 'createdAt'>): SSEClient {
    // 同项目客户端数检查
    let count = 0;
    for (const c of this.clients.values()) {
      if (c.projectId === client.projectId) count++;
    }
    if (count >= SSEManager.MAX_PER_PROJECT) {
      throw new Error(`项目 ${client.projectId} 的 SSE 客户端数已达上限(${SSEManager.MAX_PER_PROJECT})`);
    }

    client.res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream');
    client.res.setHeader('Cache-Control', 'no-cache');
    client.res.setHeader('Connection', 'keep-alive');
    client.res.setHeader('X-Accel-Buffering', 'no');  // 禁用 nginx 缓冲
    client.res.flushHeaders();

    const fullClient: SSEClient = {
      ...client,
      heartbeat: setInterval(() => {
        try {
          client.res.write(`:heartbeat ${Date.now()}\n\n`);
        } catch {
          this.unregister(client.id);
        }
      }, 30000),
      createdAt: Date.now(),
    };
    this.clients.set(client.id, fullClient);
    return fullClient;
  }

  send(id: string, event: string, data: any): boolean {
    const c = this.clients.get(id);
    if (!c) return false;
    try {
      c.res.write(`event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`);
      return true;
    } catch {
      this.unregister(id);
      return false;
    }
  }

  broadcast(projectId: number, event: string, data: any): number {
    let sent = 0;
    for (const c of this.clients.values()) {
      if (c.projectId === projectId && this.send(c.id, event, data)) {
        sent++;
      }
    }
    return sent;
  }

  unregister(id: string): boolean {
    const c = this.clients.get(id);
    if (!c) return false;
    clearInterval(c.heartbeat);  // ★ 防止心跳保活导致进程不退出
    try { c.res.end(); } catch {}
    this.clients.delete(id);
    return true;
  }

  getStats() {
    return { totalClients: this.clients.size };
  }

  // 优雅关闭
  closeAll(): void {
    for (const id of this.clients.keys()) {
      this.unregister(id);
    }
  }
}

// =====================================================
// 3. 任务追踪(防内存泄漏 - LRU 上限)
// =====================================================
interface TaskRecord {
  taskId: string;
  projectId: number;
  status: 'running' | 'completed' | 'failed';
  result?: SAOutput;
  error?: string;
  startedAt: number;
  completedAt?: number;
}

class TaskTracker {
  private tasks = new Map<string, TaskRecord>();
  // 上限 1000 条 - 防止长跑内存泄漏
  private static readonly MAX_TASKS = 1000;

  set(task: TaskRecord): void {
    if (this.tasks.size >= TaskTracker.MAX_TASKS) {
      // 删除最老的(按 startedAt)
      const oldest = [...this.tasks.entries()].sort((a, b) => a[1].startedAt - b[1].startedAt)[0];
      if (oldest) this.tasks.delete(oldest[0]);
    }
    this.tasks.set(task.taskId, task);
  }

  update(taskId: string, patch: Partial<TaskRecord>): TaskRecord | undefined {
    const t = this.tasks.get(taskId);
    if (!t) return undefined;
    Object.assign(t, patch);
    return t;
  }

  get(taskId: string): TaskRecord | undefined {
    return this.tasks.get(taskId);
  }

  // 清理 30 分钟前的已完成任务
  cleanup(maxAgeMs: number = 30 * 60 * 1000): number {
    const now = Date.now();
    let cleaned = 0;
    for (const [id, t] of this.tasks) {
      if (t.completedAt && now - t.completedAt > maxAgeMs) {
        this.tasks.delete(id);
        cleaned++;
      }
    }
    return cleaned;
  }

  getStats() {
    return {
      totalTasks: this.tasks.size,
      running: [...this.tasks.values()].filter(t => t.status === 'running').length,
      completed: [...this.tasks.values()].filter(t => t.status === 'completed').length,
      failed: [...this.tasks.values()].filter(t => t.status === 'failed').length,
    };
  }
}

// =====================================================
// 4. Express 服务器
// =====================================================
const app = express();
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }));

const PORT = parseInt(process.env.SA_SERVICE_PORT || '3001', 10);
const LLM_GATEWAY_URL = process.env.LLM_GATEWAY_URL || '';
const LLM_API_KEY = process.env.LLM_API_KEY || '';
const LLM_PROVIDER = process.env.LLM_PROVIDER || 'deepseek';
const LLM_TEMPERATURE = parseFloat(process.env.LLM_TEMPERATURE || '0.3');
const LLM_MAX_TOKENS = parseInt(process.env.LLM_MAX_TOKENS || '4096', 10);
const MAX_CONCURRENCY = parseInt(process.env.SA_MAX_CONCURRENCY || String(DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENCY), 10);
const DB_BACKEND = (process.env.SA_DB_BACKEND || 'inmemory').toLowerCase();

const sseManager = new SSEManager();
const taskTracker = new TaskTracker();

// 定期清理旧任务(每 5 分钟)
const cleanupInterval = setInterval(() => {
  const cleaned = taskTracker.cleanup();
  if (cleaned > 0) console.log(`[SA Service] cleaned ${cleaned} old tasks`);
}, 5 * 60 * 1000);

// =====================================================
// 健康检查
// =====================================================
app.get('/api/sa/health', (_req, res) => {
  res.json({
    status: 'ok',
    service: 'sa-service',
    version: '2.0.0',
    uptime: process.uptime(),
    ...sseManager.getStats(),
    ...taskTracker.getStats(),
  });
});

// =====================================================
// POST /api/sa/run — 异步启动 SA 流水线
// 立即返回 taskId,SSE 推进度
// =====================================================
app.post('/api/sa/run', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { tenantId, projectId, requirementId, requirementText, userId, industry, sseSessionId } = req.body;

    if (!requirementText || !tenantId || !projectId) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: '缺少必要参数: tenantId, projectId, requirementText' });
    }

    const taskId = uuidv4();
    taskTracker.set({
      taskId,
      projectId,
      status: 'running',
      startedAt: Date.now(),
    });

    // 立即通知开始
    if (sseSessionId) sseManager.send(sseSessionId, 'task-started', { taskId, projectId });

    // 异步执行,不阻塞 HTTP 响应
    setImmediate(() => {
      runSAsync(taskId, req.body, sseSessionId).catch(err => {
        console.error(`[SA Service] task ${taskId} failed:`, err);
      });
    });

    res.json({ taskId, status: 'running', sseSessionId: sseSessionId || null });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[SA Service] /api/sa/run error:', err);
    res.status(500).json({ error: '启动失败', message: (err as Error).message });
  }
});

// 异步执行 SA 流水线(独立函数,避免闭包泄漏)
async function runSAsync(taskId: string, body: any, sseSessionId?: string) {
  const { tenantId, projectId, requirementId, requirementText, userId, industry } = body;

  const progressFn = (event: SAProgressEvent) => {
    if (sseSessionId) {
      sseManager.send(sseSessionId, event.type, event);
    }
  };

  try {
    const authHeader = body.authHeader || '';
    const providerCode = body.providerCode || body.provider || LLM_PROVIDER;
    const llm = new HttpLlmClient(LLM_GATEWAY_URL, LLM_API_KEY, tenantId, authHeader, providerCode, LLM_TEMPERATURE, LLM_MAX_TOKENS);

    const db = createDatabase(DB_BACKEND);

    // ★ 真实 Validator 注入(不再 null)
    const validators = await buildValidators();

    const saConfig: SAConfig = {
      ...DEFAULT_SA_CONFIG,
      maxRetries: parseInt(process.env.SA_MAX_RETRIES || '3', 10),
    };

    const orchestrator = new SAOrchestrator(llm, db, validators, saConfig, MAX_CONCURRENCY);

    const saRequest: SARequest = {
      tenantId,
      projectId,
      requirementId: requirementId || 0,
      requirementText,
      userId: userId || 'anonymous',
    };
    if (industry) (saRequest as any).industry = industry;

    const result = await orchestrator.runSA(saRequest, progressFn);

    taskTracker.update(taskId, { status: 'completed', result, completedAt: Date.now() });
    if (sseSessionId) sseManager.send(sseSessionId, 'task-completed', { taskId, duration: Date.now() - (taskTracker.get(taskId)?.startedAt || Date.now()) });
  } catch (err) {
    taskTracker.update(taskId, { status: 'failed', error: (err as Error).message, completedAt: Date.now() });
    if (sseSessionId) sseManager.send(sseSessionId, 'task-failed', { taskId, error: (err as Error).message });
  }
}

// =====================================================
// POST /api/sa/run-sync — 同步执行 SA 流水线(老接口,完整 9 步返回)
// =====================================================
app.post('/api/sa/run-sync', async (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { tenantId, projectId, requirementId, requirementText, userId, industry } = req.body;
    if (!requirementText || !tenantId || !projectId) {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: '缺少必要参数' });
    }

    const authHeader = req.body.authHeader || '';
    const llm = new HttpLlmClient(LLM_GATEWAY_URL, LLM_API_KEY, tenantId, authHeader, LLM_PROVIDER, LLM_TEMPERATURE, LLM_MAX_TOKENS);
    const db = createDatabase(DB_BACKEND);
    const validators = await buildValidators();
    const orchestrator = new SAOrchestrator(llm, db, validators, DEFAULT_SA_CONFIG, MAX_CONCURRENCY);

    const saRequest: SARequest = {
      tenantId, projectId,
      requirementId: requirementId || 0,
      requirementText,
      userId: userId || 'anonymous',
    };
    if (industry) (saRequest as any).industry = industry;

    const startTime = Date.now();
    const result = await orchestrator.runSA(saRequest);
    const duration = Date.now() - startTime;

    // ★ 完整 9 步返回(不再截断到 6 步)
    res.json({
      taskId: uuidv4(),
      status: 'completed',
      duration,
      result: {
        // Project-level
        scope: result.scope,
        dfd: result.dfd,
        bpm: result.bpm,
        dict: result.dict,
        er: result.er,
        std: result.stateMachine,
        // 第一个事件的 PSpec/DT/UI(向后兼容)
        pspec: result.pspec,
        decisionTable: result.decisionTable,
        ui: result.ui,
        // ★ 新字段:每事件独立
        eventAssets: result.eventAssets ? Object.fromEntries(result.eventAssets) : {},
        eventErrors: result.eventErrors ? Object.fromEntries(result.eventErrors) : {},
        // 元数据
        metadata: result.metadata,
      },
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[SA Service] /api/sa/run-sync error:', err);
    res.status(500).json({ error: 'SA 流水线执行失败', message: (err as Error).message });
  }
});

// =====================================================
// GET /api/sa/events — SSE 事件流(防内存泄漏)
// =====================================================
app.get('/api/sa/events', (req, res) => {
  const projectId = parseInt((req.query.projectId as string) || '0', 10);
  const sessionId = req.query.sessionId as string || uuidv4();

  try {
    const client = sseManager.create({
      id: sessionId,
      projectId,
      res,
    });
    sseManager.send(sessionId, 'connected', { sessionId, projectId });

    // ★ 关键:close 和 error 都要清理
    const cleanup = () => {
      sseManager.unregister(sessionId);
    };
    req.on('close', cleanup);
    req.on('error', (err) => {
      console.error(`[SSE] session ${sessionId} error:`, err.message);
      cleanup();
    });
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[SSE] create failed:', err);
    res.status(503).json({ error: (err as Error).message });
  }
});

// =====================================================
// GET /api/sa/tasks/:taskId — 查询任务
// =====================================================
app.get('/api/sa/tasks/:taskId', (req, res) => {
  const task = taskTracker.get(req.params.taskId);
  if (!task) return res.status(404).json({ error: '任务不存在' });
  res.json({
    taskId: task.taskId,
    status: task.status,
    startedAt: new Date(task.startedAt).toISOString(),
    completedAt: task.completedAt ? new Date(task.completedAt).toISOString() : null,
    duration: task.completedAt ? task.completedAt - task.startedAt : Date.now() - task.startedAt,
    error: task.error,
    eventCount: task.result?.metadata?.eventCount,
    eventSuccessCount: task.result?.metadata?.eventSuccessCount,
    eventErrorCount: task.result?.metadata?.eventErrorCount,
  });
});

// =====================================================
// GET /api/sa/tasks/:taskId/result — 完整结果
// =====================================================
app.get('/api/sa/tasks/:taskId/result', (req, res) => {
  const task = taskTracker.get(req.params.taskId);
  if (!task) return res.status(404).json({ error: '任务不存在' });
  if (task.status !== 'completed') return res.status(409).json({ error: '任务未完成', status: task.status });
  res.json(task.result);
});

// =====================================================
// 优雅关闭(防内存泄漏)
// =====================================================
const shutdown = () => {
  console.log('[SA Service] shutting down...');
  sseManager.closeAll();
  clearInterval(cleanupInterval);
  process.exit(0);
};
process.on('SIGTERM', shutdown);
process.on('SIGINT', shutdown);

// =====================================================
// 启动
// =====================================================
app.listen(PORT, () => {
  if (!LLM_GATEWAY_URL) console.error('[SA Service] ⚠️  LLM_GATEWAY_URL 未设置');
  if (DB_BACKEND === 'inmemory') console.warn('[SA Service] ⚠️  使用 InMemory DB(重启丢数据)');
  console.log(`[SA Service] v2.0.0 启动完成 → 端口:${PORT}, DB:${DB_BACKEND}, Provider:${LLM_PROVIDER}, Concurrency:${MAX_CONCURRENCY}`);
  if (LLM_GATEWAY_URL) console.log(`[SA Service] LLM Gateway → ${LLM_GATEWAY_URL}`);
});

// =====================================================
// 辅助函数
// =====================================================
import { ISADatabase } from './orchestrator/orchestrator-types';
import { SqlServerSADatabase } from './orchestrator/SqlServerSADatabase';
import { ConsoleLogger } from './orchestrator/Logger';
import sql from 'mssql';

function createDatabase(backend: string): ISADatabase {
  if (backend === 'sqlserver') {
    // ★ 生产: SQL Server + 离线队列
    const poolFactory = async () => {
      const config: sql.config = {
        server: process.env.DB_HOST || 'localhost',
        port: parseInt(process.env.DB_PORT || '1433', 10),
        database: process.env.DB_NAME || 'sa_db',
        user: process.env.DB_USER || 'sa',
        password: process.env.DB_PASSWORD || '',
        options: { encrypt: false, trustServerCertificate: true },
        pool: { max: 10, min: 0, idleTimeoutMillis: 30000 },
      };
      return await sql.connect(config) as any;
    };
    return new SqlServerSADatabase(poolFactory, {
      enableOfflineQueue: true,
      queueDir: process.env.SA_OFFLINE_QUEUE_DIR || './sa-offline-queue',
      logger: new ConsoleLogger('SA-DB'),
    });
  }
  return new InMemorySADatabase();
}

// ★ 真实 Validator 注入(动态 import 避免硬依赖)
async function buildValidators(): Promise<ValidatorBundle> {
  try {
    // 尝试从本地 validators 包加载
    const validatorsModule = await import('./validators' as any).catch(() => null);
    if (validatorsModule) {
      return {
        DFDValidator: validatorsModule.DFDValidator,
        BPMValidator: validatorsModule.BPMValidator,
        DictValidator: validatorsModule.DictValidator,
        LogicValidator: validatorsModule.LogicValidator,
        CrossEventConsistencyValidator: validatorsModule.CrossEventConsistencyValidator,
        ERValidator: validatorsModule.ERValidator,
        STDReachabilityValidator: validatorsModule.STDReachabilityValidator,
        UIValidator: validatorsModule.UIValidator,
      };
    }
  } catch {}
  // fallback - 返回 null(允许跳过校验,仅开发用)
  console.warn('[SA Service] 未找到 validators 包,使用 null 注入(无校验模式)');
  return {
    DFDValidator: null as any, BPMValidator: null as any, DictValidator: null as any,
    LogicValidator: null as any, CrossEventConsistencyValidator: null as any,
    ERValidator: null as any, STDReachabilityValidator: null as any, UIValidator: null as any,
  };
}

export { app };

=== FILE: ./sa-simple/README.md ===
# SA 简化版 - 9 步流水线

**针对 AI 工程师能力不足的场景**,把之前过度设计的 SAOrchestrator 简化成"9 个顺序函数"。

## 设计原则

| 之前(过度设计) | 现在(简化版) |
|---|---|
| SAOrchestrator + 3-Tier 路由 | ❌ 删 |
| Semaphore 并发限流 | ❌ 删 |
| 并行事件处理 | ❌ 删 |
| SqlServerSADatabase + 离线队列 | ❌ 删 |
| 9 个 Agent class + buildPrompt | ❌ 删 |
| 7 个 Validator | ❌ 删 |
| DKEE Pattern 提炼 | ❌ 删 |
| RetryLoop 错误回灌 | ❌ 删 |
| **9 个顺序函数** | ✅ **保留** |
| **9 个 LLM Prompt(纯字符串)** | ✅ **保留** |
| **10 张 SA 表 INSERT** | ✅ **保留** |
| **Markdown 文档生成** | ✅ **新增** |

## 文件结构(5 个核心文件)

```
sa-simple/
├── src/
│   ├── index.ts        # 导出入口
│   ├── runSA.ts        # ★ 9 步流水线主函数(60 行)
│   ├── prompts.ts      # ★ 9 个 LLM Prompt(200 行,纯字符串)
│   ├── db.ts           # ★ 9 个 save 函数(每个 ~20 行)
│   ├── llm.ts          # LLM 调用(40 行)
│   └── document.ts     # Markdown 文档生成(150 行)
├── package.json
├── tsconfig.json
└── README.md
```

**总代码量: ~600 行**(对比之前 SDK 1300 行)

## 9 步流水线(主函数)

```typescript
export async function runSA(req: SARequest): Promise<SAOutput> {
  // Step 1: Scope
  const scope = await callLLM(SCOPE_PROMPT, req.requirementText);
  const scopeId = await saveScope({...scope}, req.userId);

  // Step 2: DFD
  const dfd = await callLLM(DFD_PROMPT, { requirement: req.requirementText, scope });
  const dfdId = await saveDFD({...dfd, scopeId}, req.userId);

  // ... 一共 9 步,完全类似
}
```

**就这么多**。每步 = 1 个 prompt + 1 次 LLM + 1 次 INSERT。

## 使用方法

```typescript
import { initLLM, initDb, runSA, generateMarkdownDoc } from 'sa-simple';

// 1. 初始化
initLLM({
  apiKey: process.env.OPENAI_API_KEY!,
  baseUrl: 'https://api.openai.com/v1',
  model: 'gpt-4',
});

await initDb({
  server: 'localhost',
  port: 1433,
  database: 'sa_db',
  user: 'sa',
  password: 'StrongPass123!',
});

// 2. 跑 9 步(顺序,~30 秒)
const result = await runSA({
  tenantId: 'tenant_001',
  projectId: 1001,
  requirementText: 'MES 报工系统,机加工车间,工人提交工序报工,班组长终核...',
  userId: 'analyst_001',
});

// 3. 生成 Markdown 文档
const doc = generateMarkdownDoc(result);
console.log(doc);  // 200 页的系统需求分析说明书
```

## 9 步的内容(详细)

| Step | 表 | 核心字段(JSON) | LLM 提示词 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1. Scope | sa_scope | systemBoundary, externalEntities, businessEvents | 提取系统边界和业务事件 |
| 2. DFD | sa_dfd | contextDiagram, dfdLevels, processes, dataFlows, dataStores | 生成数据流图 |
| 3. BPM | sa_business_process | swimLanes, activityNodes, edges, dfdProcessMappings | 生成泳道流程图 |
| 4. Dict | sa_data_dictionary | elements, dataFlows, dataStores | 字段定义(含类型白名单) |
| 5. PSPEC | sa_pspec | processSpecs | 过程规格 |
| 6. DT | sa_decision_table | tables | 业务规则判定表 |
| 7. ER | sa_er | entities, relationships | 实体关系图 |
| 8. STD | sa_state_machine | stateMachines | 状态机(STD) |
| 9. UI | sa_ui | screens | UI 屏原型 |

## 给 AI 工程师的指南

每步只需要:
1. 复制对应 Prompt 到 LLM 测试
2. 准备输入数据
3. 跑 LLM 看输出格式
4. 调 Prompt 直到输出符合 JSON Schema

**调试单步的成本: 5-10 分钟**(直接 LLM Playground 跑)
**AI 工程师实施难度: 低**(就是字符串拼接 + SQL INSERT)

## 输出: 系统需求分析说明书

`generateMarkdownDoc(result)` 把 9 步产出组合成一份 Markdown:

```markdown
# 《MES 报工系统需求分析说明书》

## 1. 系统范围与边界
## 2. 数据流图 (DFD)
## 3. 业务流程图 (BPM)
## 4. 数据字典 (★ 关键)
## 5. 过程规格 (PSPEC)
## 6. 业务规则 (判定表)
## 7. ER 图
## 8. 状态机
## 9. UI 原型
```

## 数据全部入库

每步输出都 `INSERT INTO sa_xxx`,9 张表都有最新数据:

```sql
SELECT * FROM sa_scope WHERE project_id = 1001;       -- Step 1
SELECT * FROM sa_dfd WHERE project_id = 1001;         -- Step 2
SELECT * FROM sa_data_dictionary WHERE project_id = 1001;  -- Step 4
...
```

**断电不丢**(SQL Server 有 WAL)。
**二次开发**:人改 dict,只 UPDATE 那一行,其他 8 步不动。

## 什么时候用"简化版" vs "完整版"?

| 场景 | 推荐 |
|---|---|
| AI 工程师能力不够 | **简化版**(本目录) |
| 演示 / POC | **简化版** |
| 多个需求文档批量分析 | 完整版(并行) |
| 多事件并发处理 | 完整版(3-Tier) |
| 跨事件一致性要求 | 完整版(CrossEvent Validator) |

**生产环境建议先用简化版跑通,再按需升级到完整版。**

## 升级路径(从简化版到完整版)

如果以后 AI 工程师能力上来了,可以逐步加:
1. Validator:每步 LLM 输出后跑校验,失败 retry
2. DKEE:跑完 9 步后提炼 Pattern
3. 并行:多个需求文档同时分析(Semaphore)
4. 3-Tier:大项目分 PROJECT / EVENT / PROCESS

每次只加一个能力,AI 工程师慢慢消化。

// =====================================================
// SA 简化版 DB 写入(已加事务 + 幂等支持)
// =====================================================

import * as sql from 'mssql';

let pool: sql.ConnectionPool | null = null;

export async function initDb(config: {
  server: string; port: number; database: string; user: string; password: string;
}): Promise<void> {
  pool = await sql.connect({
    server: config.server,
    port: config.port,
    database: config.database,
    user: config.user,
    password: config.password,
    options: { encrypt: false, trustServerCertificate: true },
  });
  console.log('✓ DB connected');
}

export async function getPool(): Promise<sql.ConnectionPool> {
  if (!pool) throw new Error('DB not initialized');
  return pool;
}

// =====================================================
// 事务包装(P0-2)
// =====================================================
export async function withTransaction<T>(
  fn: (trx: sql.Transaction) => Promise<T>
): Promise<T> {
  const p = await getPool();
  const trx = new sql.Transaction(p);
  await trx.begin();
  try {
    const result = await fn(trx);
    await trx.commit();
    return result;
  } catch (e) {
    try { await trx.rollback(); } catch { /* ignore */ }
    throw e;
  }
}

// 通用 SQL 执行 - 支持 transaction
async function exec(sqlText: string, params: Record<string, any>, trx?: sql.Transaction): Promise<number> {
  const req = trx
    ? new sql.Request(trx)
    : new sql.Request(await getPool());
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(params)) req.input(k, v);
  const result = await req.query(sqlText);
  return result.recordset[0].id;
}

// =====================================================
// Step 1: Scope
// =====================================================
export async function saveScope(input: {
  tenantId: string; projectId: number;
  systemBoundary: any; externalEntities: any[]; businessEvents: any[];
}, userId: string, trx?: sql.Transaction): Promise<number> {
  return exec(
    `INSERT INTO sa_scope
      (tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, system_boundary, external_entities, business_events, event_count,
       validation_status, human_confirmed, created_by, updated_by)
     OUTPUT INSERTED.id
     VALUES
      (@tenantId, @projectId, 'PROJECT', @systemBoundary, @externalEntities, @businessEvents, @eventCount,
       'PASS', 0, @userId, @userId)`,
    {
      tenantId: input.tenantId, projectId: input.projectId,
      systemBoundary: JSON.stringify(input.systemBoundary),
      externalEntities: JSON.stringify(input.externalEntities),
      businessEvents: JSON.stringify(input.businessEvents),
      eventCount: input.businessEvents.length,
      userId,
    },
    trx
  );
}

// =====================================================

// =====================================================
export async function saveDFD(input: {
  tenantId: string; projectId: number; scopeId: number;
  contextDiagram: any; dfdLevels: any; processes: any[]; dataFlows: any[]; dataStores: any[];
}, userId: string, trx?: sql.Transaction): Promise<number> {
  return exec(
    `INSERT INTO sa_dfd
      (tenant_id, project_id, asset_level, scope_id, context_diagram, dfd_levels, processes, data_flows, data_stores,
       validation_status, human_confirmed, created_by, updated_by)
     OUTPUT INSERTED.id
     VALUES
      (@tenantId, @projectId, 'PROJECT', @scopeId, @contextDiagram, @dfdLevels, @processes, @dataFlows, @dataStores,
       'PASS', 0, @userId, @userId)`,
    {
      tenantId: input.tenantId, projectId: input.projectId, scopeId: input.scopeId,
      contextDiagram: JSON.stringify(input.contextDiagram),
      dfdLevels: JSON.stringify(input.dfdLevels),
      processes: JSON.stringify(input.processes),
      dataFlows: JSON.stringify(input.dataFlows),
      dataStores: JSON.stringify(input.dataStores),
      userId,
    },
    trx
  );
}

// =====================================================
// 幂等性支持(P0-3)
// =====================================================
export async function findExistingAnalysis(
  tenantId: string, projectId: number, reqHash: string
): Promise<any | null> {
  const p = await getPool();
  const result = await p.request()
    .input('tenantId', tenantId)
    .input('projectId', projectId)
    .input('reqHash', reqHash)
    .query(`
      SELECT TOP 1 result_json, status
      FROM sa_analysis_runs
      WHERE tenant_id = @tenantId
        AND project_id = @projectId
        AND req_hash = @reqHash
        AND status = 'COMPLETED'
      ORDER BY created_at DESC
    `);
  if (result.recordset.length === 0) return null;
  return JSON.parse(result.recordset[0].result_json);
}

export async function recordAnalysisRun(
  tenantId: string, projectId: number, reqHash: string,
  result: any, status: 'COMPLETED' | 'FAILED' = 'COMPLETED',
  errorMsg?: string, trx?: sql.Transaction
): Promise<number> {
  return exec(
    `INSERT INTO sa_analysis_runs
      (tenant_id, project_id, req_hash, status, result_json, error_msg, created_at, updated_at)
     OUTPUT INSERTED.id
     VALUES
      (@tenantId, @projectId, @reqHash, @status, @resultJson, @errorMsg, GETDATE(), GETDATE())`,
    {
      tenantId, projectId, reqHash, status,
      resultJson: JSON.stringify(result),
      errorMsg: errorMsg || null,
    },
    trx
  );
}

// =====================================================
// 读取工具(供 document.ts 生成最终说明书)
// =====================================================
export async function getFullSA(tenantId: string, projectId: number): Promise<{
  scope: any; dfd: any; bpm: any; dict: any; pspec: any; dt: any; er: any; std: any; ui: any;
}> {
  const p = await getPool();
  const r = (table: string) => p.request()
    .input('tenantId', tenantId).input('projectId', projectId)
    .query(`SELECT TOP 1 * FROM ${table} WHERE tenant_id = @tenantId AND project_id = @projectId AND is_current = 1 ORDER BY created_at DESC`);

  const [scope, dfd, bpm, dict, pspec, dt, er, sm, ui] = await Promise.all([
    r('sa_scope'), r('sa_dfd'), r('sa_business_process'), r('sa_data_dictionary'),
    r('sa_pspec'), r('sa_decision_table'), r('sa_er'), r('sa_state_machine'), r('sa_ui'),
  ]);
  return {
    scope: scope.recordset[0] || null,
    dfd: dfd.recordset[0] || null,
    bpm: bpm.recordset[0] || null,
    dict: dict.recordset[0] || null,
    pspec: pspec.recordset[0] || null,
    dt: dt.recordset[0] || null,
    er: er.recordset[0] || null,
    std: sm.recordset[0] || null,
    ui: ui.recordset[0] || null,
  };
}

export async function closeDb(): Promise<void> {
  if (pool) {
    await pool.close();
    pool = null;
  }
}

=== FILE: ./sa-simple/src/document.ts ===
// =====================================================
// 把 9 步产出组合成一份 Markdown 系统需求分析说明书
// =====================================================

export function generateMarkdownDoc(saOutput: any): string {
  const md: string[] = [];
  const title = `《${saOutput.scope?.systemBoundary?.inScope?.[0] || '系统'}需求分析说明书》`;

  md.push(`# ${title}`);
  md.push('');
  md.push(`> 生成时间: ${new Date().toISOString()}`);
  md.push(`> 项目 ID: ${saOutput.projectId}`);
  md.push(`> 租户: ${saOutput.tenantId}`);
  md.push('');

  // ============ 1. 范围 ============
  md.push('## 1. 系统范围与边界');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 1.1 本次实现范围 (In Scope)');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.scope?.systemBoundary?.inScope || []).forEach((s: string) => md.push(`- ${s}`));
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 1.2 不在本次范围 (Out of Scope)');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.scope?.systemBoundary?.outOfScope || []).forEach((s: string) => md.push(`- ${s}`));
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 1.3 外部实体');
  md.push('');
  md.push('| 实体 | 类型 | 说明 |');
  md.push('|------|------|------|');
  (saOutput.scope?.externalEntities || []).forEach((e: any) => {
    md.push(`| ${e.name} | ${e.type} | ${e.description || ''} |`);
  });
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 1.4 业务事件清单');
  md.push('');
  md.push('| ID | 事件名 | 复杂度 | 说明 |');
  md.push('|----|--------|--------|------|');
  (saOutput.scope?.businessEvents || []).forEach((e: any) => {
    md.push(`| ${e.id} | ${e.name} | ${e.complexity} | ${e.description || ''} |`);
  });
  md.push('');

  // ============ 2. 数据流图 ============
  md.push('## 2. 数据流图 (DFD)');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 2.1 顶层 Context 图');
  md.push('```json');
  md.push(JSON.stringify(saOutput.dfd?.contextDiagram || {}, null, 2));
  md.push('```');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 2.2 分层分解');
  md.push('```json');
  md.push(JSON.stringify(saOutput.dfd?.dfdLevels || {}, null, 2));
  md.push('```');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 2.3 加工过程');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.dfd?.processes || []).forEach((p: any) => {
    md.push(`#### ${p.id} - ${p.name}`);
    md.push(`- 父过程: ${p.parentId || '(无,顶层)'}`);
    md.push(`- 输入流: ${(p.inputFlows || []).join(', ') || '(无)'}`);
    md.push(`- 输出流: ${(p.outputFlows || []).join(', ') || '(无)'}`);
    md.push('');
  });
  md.push('### 2.4 数据流');
  md.push('```json');
  md.push(JSON.stringify(saOutput.dfd?.dataFlows || [], null, 2));
  md.push('```');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 2.5 数据存储');
  md.push('```json');
  md.push(JSON.stringify(saOutput.dfd?.dataStores || [], null, 2));
  md.push('```');
  md.push('');

  // ============ 3. 业务流程图 ============
  md.push('## 3. 业务流程图 (BPM)');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 3.1 泳道');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.bpm?.swimLanes || []).forEach((l: any) => md.push(`- **${l.name}** (${l.role})`));
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 3.2 活动节点');
  md.push('');
  md.push('| 节点 ID | 名称 | 泳道 | 类型 | DFD 过程 |');
  md.push('|---------|------|------|------|----------|');
  (saOutput.bpm?.activityNodes || []).forEach((n: any) => {
    md.push(`| ${n.id} | ${n.name} | ${n.laneId} | ${n.type} | ${n.dfdProcessId || ''} |`);
  });
  md.push('');

  // ============ 4. 数据字典 ============
  md.push('## 4. 数据字典 (★ 关键)');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 4.1 字段定义');
  md.push('');
  md.push('| 字段名 | 类型 | 主键 | 外键 | 引用实体 | 必填 |');
  md.push('|--------|------|------|------|----------|------|');
  (saOutput.dict?.elements || []).forEach((e: any) => {
    md.push(`| ${e.name} | ${e.type} | ${e.isPK ? '✓' : ''} | ${e.isFK ? '✓' : ''} | ${e.refEntity || ''} | ${e.isRequired ? '✓' : ''} |`);
  });
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 4.2 数据存储');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.dict?.dataStores || []).forEach((ds: any) => {
    md.push(`#### ${ds.name}`);
    md.push('');
    md.push('| 字段 | 类型 |');
    md.push('|------|------|');
    (ds.fields || []).forEach((f: any) => {
      md.push(`| ${f.name} | ${f.type} |`);
    });
    md.push('');
  });

  // ============ 5. 过程规格 ============
  md.push('## 5. 过程规格 (PSPEC)');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.pspec?.processSpecs || []).forEach((p: any) => {
    md.push(`### ${p.id} - ${p.name}`);
    md.push(`- 输入: ${(p.input || []).join(', ')}`);
    md.push(`- 输出: ${(p.output || []).join(', ')}`);
    if (p.validation) md.push(`- 校验: ${p.validation}`);
    if (p.algorithm) md.push(`- 算法: ${p.algorithm}`);
    md.push('');
  });

  // ============ 6. 判定表 ============
  md.push('## 6. 业务规则 (判定表)');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.decisionTable?.tables || []).forEach((t: any) => {
    md.push(`### ${t.id}`);
    md.push('');
    md.push('**条件:**');
    md.push('');
    (t.conditions || []).forEach((c: any, i: number) => {
      md.push(`${i + 1}. ${c.name} ${c.operator} ${JSON.stringify(c.value)}`);
    });
    md.push('');
    md.push('**动作:**');
    md.push('');
    (t.actions || []).forEach((a: any, i: number) => {
      md.push(`${i}. ${a.name}`);
    });
    md.push('');
    md.push('**规则矩阵:**');
    md.push('');
    md.push('| ' + (t.conditions || []).map((c: any) => c.name).join(' | ') + ' | 动作 |');
    md.push('|' + (t.conditions || []).map(() => '---').join('|') + '|------|');
    (t.rules || []).forEach((r: any) => {
      const condStr = (r.conditionMask || []).map((m: boolean) => m ? '✓' : '✗').join(' | ');
      const action = t.actions[r.actionIndex]?.name || `规则${r.actionIndex}`;
      md.push(`| ${condStr} | ${action} |`);
    });
    md.push('');
  });

  // ============ 7. ER 图 ============
  md.push('## 7. ER 图');
  md.push('');
  md.push('### 7.1 实体');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.er?.entities || []).forEach((e: any) => {
    md.push(`#### ${e.name}`);
    md.push('');
    md.push('| 字段 | 类型 | 主键 | 外键 | 引用表 |');
    md.push('|------|------|------|------|--------|');
    (e.columns || []).forEach((c: any) => {
      md.push(`| ${c.name} | ${c.type} | ${c.isPK ? '✓' : ''} | ${c.isFK ? '✓' : ''} | ${c.refTable || ''} |`);
    });
    md.push('');
  });
  md.push('### 7.2 关系');
  md.push('');
  md.push('| 源实体 | 目标实体 | 类型 | 外键列 |');
  md.push('|--------|----------|------|--------|');
  (saOutput.er?.relationships || []).forEach((r: any) => {
    md.push(`| ${r.from} | ${r.to} | ${r.type} | ${r.fkColumn || ''} |`);
  });
  md.push('');

  // ============ 8. 状态机 ============
  md.push('## 8. 状态机');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.stateMachine?.stateMachines || []).forEach((sm: any) => {
    md.push(`### ${sm.entity}`);
    md.push('');
    md.push('**状态:** ' + (sm.states || []).join(' / '));
    md.push('');
    md.push('**转换:**');
    md.push('');
    md.push('| from | to | trigger |');
    md.push('|------|----|---------|');
    (sm.transitions || []).forEach((t: any) => {
      md.push(`| ${t.from} | ${t.to} | ${t.trigger} |`);
    });
    md.push('');
  });

  // ============ 9. UI 原型 ============
  md.push('## 9. UI 原型');
  md.push('');
  (saOutput.ui?.screens || []).forEach((s: any) => {
    md.push(`### ${s.id} - ${s.name}`);
    md.push('');
    md.push(`数据流: ${s.dataFlow} | BPM 节点: ${s.bpmNodeId}`);
    md.push('');
    md.push('| 字段 | 类型 | 必填 | 控件 |');
    md.push('|------|------|------|------|');
    (s.fields || []).forEach((f: any) => {
      md.push(`| ${f.name} | ${f.type} | ${f.required ? '✓' : ''} | ${f.controlType} |`);
    });
    md.push('');
  });

  md.push('---');
  md.push(`*本文档由 SA 流水线自动生成,共 9 步:*`);
  md.push(`*1. Scope | 2. DFD | 3. BPM | 4. Dict | 5. PSPEC | 6. DecisionTable | 7. ER | 8. StateMachine | 9. UI*`);

  return md.join('\n');
}

=== FILE: ./sa-simple/src/guards.ts ===
// =====================================================
// Schema Guard - 9 个纯函数,每个 Step 一个
// 原则: LLM 输出必须过 guard 才能进 save 函数
// 检查: 类型 + 必要字段,不做业务规则验证
// 出错信息明确告诉 prompt engineer "哪步、缺什么字段"
// =====================================================

// 工具函数
function ensureString(val: any, fieldName: string): string {
  if (typeof val !== 'string' || val.trim() === '') {
    throw new Error(
      `LLM 输出缺必要字段 ${fieldName}(应是字符串),实际: ${JSON.stringify(val)?.slice(0, 200)}`
    );
  }
  return val;
}

function ensureNumber(val: any, fieldName: string): number {
  if (typeof val !== 'number' || isNaN(val)) {
    throw new Error(`LLM 输出缺必要字段 ${fieldName}(应是数字),实际: ${JSON.stringify(val)?.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return val;
}

function ensureArray(val: any, fieldName: string): any[] {
  if (!Array.isArray(val)) {
    throw new Error(`LLM 输出缺必要字段 ${fieldName}(应是数组),实际: ${JSON.stringify(val)?.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return val;
}

function ensureObject(val: any, fieldName: string): any {
  if (!val || typeof val !== 'object' || Array.isArray(val)) {
    throw new Error(`LLM 输出缺必要字段 ${fieldName}(应是对象),实际: ${JSON.stringify(val)?.slice(0, 200)}`);
  }
  return val;
}

// =====================================================
// Step 1: Scope
// =====================================================
export interface ScopeOutput {
  systemBoundary: { inScope: string[]; outOfScope: string[] };
  externalEntities: Array<{ name: string; type: string; description?: string }>;
  businessEvents: Array<{ id: number; name: string; description?: string; complexity: string }>;
}

export function guardScope(raw: any): ScopeOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step1 Scope');

  const sb = ensureObject(raw.systemBoundary, 'systemBoundary');
  ensureArray(sb.inScope, 'systemBoundary.inScope');
  ensureArray(sb.outOfScope, 'systemBoundary.outOfScope');

  const events = ensureArray(raw.businessEvents, 'businessEvents');
  if (events.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step1: businessEvents 至少要有 1 个业务事件');
  }
  events.forEach((e: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(e.name, `businessEvents[${i}].name`);
    if (!['simple', 'medium', 'complex'].includes(e.complexity)) {
      throw new Error(`Step1: businessEvents[${i}].complexity 必须是 simple/medium/complex,实际: ${e.complexity}`);
    }
  });

  return {
    systemBoundary: {
      inScope: sb.inScope.map((s: any) => ensureString(s, 'inScope item')),
      outOfScope: sb.outOfScope.map((s: any) => ensureString(s, 'outOfScope item')),
    },
    externalEntities: ensureArray(raw.externalEntities, 'externalEntities'),
    businessEvents: events,
  };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 2: DFD
// =====================================================
export interface DFDOutput {
  contextDiagram: any;
  dfdLevels: any;
  processes: Array<{ id: string; name: string; inputFlows: string[]; outputFlows: string[]; parentId?: string }>;
  dataFlows: any[];
  dataStores: any[];
}

export function guardDFD(raw: any): DFDOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step2 DFD');

  const processes = ensureArray(raw.processes, 'processes');
  if (processes.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step2: processes 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  processes.forEach((p: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(p.id, `processes[${i}].id`);
    ensureString(p.name, `processes[${i}].name`);
  });

  return {
    contextDiagram: ensureObject(raw.contextDiagram, 'contextDiagram'),
    dfdLevels: ensureObject(raw.dfdLevels, 'dfdLevels'),
    processes,
    dataFlows: ensureArray(raw.dataFlows, 'dataFlows'),
    dataStores: ensureArray(raw.dataStores, 'dataStores'),
  };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 3: BPM
// =====================================================
export interface BPMOutput {
  swimLanes: any[];
  activityNodes: Array<{ id: string; name: string; laneId: string; dfdProcessId?: string; type: string }>;
  edges: any[];
  exceptionPaths: any[];
  dfdProcessMappings: Record<string, string>;
}

export function guardBPM(raw: any): BPMOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step3 BPM');

  const nodes = ensureArray(raw.activityNodes, 'activityNodes');
  if (nodes.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step3: activityNodes 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  nodes.forEach((n: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(n.id, `activityNodes[${i}].id`);
    ensureString(n.name, `activityNodes[${i}].name`);
    ensureString(n.laneId, `activityNodes[${i}].laneId`);
  });

  return {
    swimLanes: ensureArray(raw.swimLanes, 'swimLanes'),
    activityNodes: nodes,
    edges: ensureArray(raw.edges, 'edges'),
    exceptionPaths: ensureArray(raw.exceptionPaths, 'exceptionPaths'),
    dfdProcessMappings: ensureObject(raw.dfdProcessMappings, 'dfdProcessMappings'),
  };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 4: Dict(★最关键)
// =====================================================
export interface DictOutput {
  elements: Array<{ name: string; type: string; isPK?: boolean; isFK?: boolean; refEntity?: string; isRequired?: boolean }>;
  dataFlows: any[];
  dataStores: any[];
}

const VALID_TYPES = ['NVARCHAR', 'BIGINT', 'INT', 'DECIMAL', 'DATETIME', 'BOOLEAN', 'JSON'];

export function guardDict(raw: any): DictOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step4 Dict');

  const elements = ensureArray(raw.elements, 'elements');
  if (elements.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step4: elements 至少要有 1 个字段');
  }

  elements.forEach((e: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(e.name, `elements[${i}].name`);
    const t = ensureString(e.type, `elements[${i}].type`);
    const baseType = t.split('(')[0];
    if (!VALID_TYPES.includes(baseType)) {
      throw new Error(`Step4: elements[${i}].type "${t}" 不在白名单 [${VALID_TYPES.join('/')}]`);
    }
  });

  // 必含审计字段
  const elementNames = new Set(elements.map((e: any) => e.name));
  ['TenantId', 'CreatedAt', 'CreatedBy', 'UpdatedAt', 'UpdatedBy'].forEach(audit => {
    if (!elementNames.has(audit)) {
      throw new Error(`Step4: 缺审计字段 "${audit}"(LLM 必须输出多租户必备字段)`);
    }
  });

  return {
    elements,
    dataFlows: ensureArray(raw.dataFlows, 'dataFlows'),
    dataStores: ensureArray(raw.dataStores, 'dataStores'),
  };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 5: PSPEC
// =====================================================
export interface PSpecOutput {
  processSpecs: Array<{ id: string; name: string; input: string[]; output: string[]; validation?: string; algorithm?: string }>;
}

export function guardPSpec(raw: any, dict?: DictOutput): PSpecOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step5 PSPEC');

  const specs = ensureArray(raw.processSpecs, 'processSpecs');
  if (specs.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step5: processSpecs 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  specs.forEach((s: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(s.id, `processSpecs[${i}].id`);
    ensureString(s.name, `processSpecs[${i}].name`);
    ensureArray(s.input, `processSpecs[${i}].input`);
    ensureArray(s.output, `processSpecs[${i}].output`);

    // 如果传了 dict,校验 input/output 字段都在 dict 里
    // input 应该是字段名;output 可以是字段名或 data store 名
    if (dict) {
      const fieldNames = new Set(
        dict.elements.map((e: any) => e.name)
      );
      const dsFieldNames = new Set(
        dict.dataStores.flatMap((ds: any) => (ds.fields || []).map((f: any) => f.name))
      );
      const dataStoreNames = new Set(
        dict.dataStores.map((ds: any) => ds.name)
      );
      [...(s.input || [])].forEach((f: any) => {
        if (!fieldNames.has(f) && !dsFieldNames.has(f)) {
          throw new Error(`Step5: processSpecs[${i}].input 引用了字典外字段 "${f}"`);
        }
      });
      [...(s.output || [])].forEach((f: any) => {
        if (!fieldNames.has(f) && !dataStoreNames.has(f)) {
          throw new Error(`Step5: processSpecs[${i}].output 引用了字典外字段/data store "${f}"`);
        }
      });
    }
  });

  return { processSpecs: specs };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 6: DecisionTable
// =====================================================
export interface DecisionTableOutput {
  tables: Array<{
    id: string;
    conditions: Array<{ name: string; operator: string; value: any }>;
    actions: Array<{ name: string }>;
    rules: Array<{ conditionMask: boolean[]; actionIndex: number }>;
  }>;
}

export function guardDecisionTable(raw: any): DecisionTableOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step6 DecisionTable');

  const tables = ensureArray(raw.tables, 'tables');
  if (tables.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step6: tables 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  tables.forEach((t: any, ti: number) => {
    ensureString(t.id, `tables[${ti}].id`);
    const conds = ensureArray(t.conditions, `tables[${ti}].conditions`);
    const actions = ensureArray(t.actions, `tables[${ti}].actions`);
    if (actions.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(`Step6: tables[${ti}].actions 至少要有 1 个`);
    }
    const rules = ensureArray(t.rules, `tables[${ti}].rules`);

    // 规则校验:conditionMask 长度 = conditions 长度
    rules.forEach((r: any, ri: number) => {
      if (!Array.isArray(r.conditionMask) || r.conditionMask.length !== conds.length) {
        throw new Error(`Step6: tables[${ti}].rules[${ri}].conditionMask 长度必须 = conditions 长度`);
      }
      if (typeof r.actionIndex !== 'number' || r.actionIndex < 0 || r.actionIndex >= actions.length) {
        throw new Error(`Step6: tables[${ti}].rules[${ri}].actionIndex 越界`);
      }
    });

    // 必含默认规则(全 false 兜底)
    const hasDefault = rules.some((r: any) =>
      Array.isArray(r.conditionMask) && r.conditionMask.every((m: boolean) => !m)
    );
    if (!hasDefault) {
      throw new Error(`Step6: tables[${ti}] 缺默认规则(所有条件都为 false 的兜底动作)`);
    }
  });

  return { tables };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 7: ER
// =====================================================
export interface EROutput {
  entities: Array<{ name: string; columns: Array<{ name: string; type: string; isPK?: boolean; isFK?: boolean; refTable?: string }> }>;
  relationships: Array<{ from: string; to: string; type: string; fkColumn?: string }>;
}

export function guardER(raw: any): EROutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step7 ER');

  const entities = ensureArray(raw.entities, 'entities');
  if (entities.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step7: entities 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  const entityNames = new Set<string>();
  entities.forEach((e: any, i: number) => {
    const name = ensureString(e.name, `entities[${i}].name`);
    if (entityNames.has(name)) {
      throw new Error(`Step7: 重复实体名 "${name}"`);
    }
    entityNames.add(name);
    const cols = ensureArray(e.columns, `entities[${i}].columns`);
    if (cols.length === 0) {
      throw new Error(`Step7: entities[${i}].columns 至少要有 1 个`);
    }
    cols.forEach((c: any, ci: number) => {
      ensureString(c.name, `entities[${i}].columns[${ci}].name`);
      ensureString(c.type, `entities[${i}].columns[${ci}].type`);
    });
  });

  // 关系里的 from/to 必须引用已知实体
  ensureArray(raw.relationships, 'relationships').forEach((r: any, ri: number) => {
    if (!entityNames.has(r.from)) {
      throw new Error(`Step7: relationships[${ri}].from "${r.from}" 不在 entities 中`);
    }
    if (!entityNames.has(r.to)) {
      throw new Error(`Step7: relationships[${ri}].to "${r.to}" 不在 entities 中`);
    }
  });

  return { entities, relationships: raw.relationships };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 8: StateMachine
// =====================================================
export interface StateMachineOutput {
  stateMachines: Array<{
    entity: string;
    states: string[];
    transitions: Array<{ from: string; to: string; trigger: string }>;
  }>;
}

export function guardStateMachine(raw: any): StateMachineOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step8 StateMachine');

  const machines = ensureArray(raw.stateMachines, 'stateMachines');
  if (machines.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step8: stateMachines 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  machines.forEach((sm: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(sm.entity, `stateMachines[${i}].entity`);
    const states = ensureArray(sm.states, `stateMachines[${i}].states`);
    if (states.length < 2) {
      throw new Error(`Step8: stateMachines[${i}].states 至少要有 2 个状态`);
    }
    const transitions = ensureArray(sm.transitions, `stateMachines[${i}].transitions`);
    transitions.forEach((t: any, ti: number) => {
      if (!states.includes(t.from)) {
        throw new Error(`Step8: stateMachines[${i}].transitions[${ti}].from "${t.from}" 不在 states 中`);
      }
      if (!states.includes(t.to)) {
        throw new Error(`Step8: stateMachines[${i}].transitions[${ti}].to "${t.to}" 不在 states 中`);
      }
      ensureString(t.trigger, `stateMachines[${i}].transitions[${ti}].trigger`);
    });

    // 必须有起始状态(无入向转换)
    const hasIncoming = new Set(transitions.map((t: any) => t.to));
    const hasInitial = states.some((s: string) => !hasIncoming.has(s));
    if (!hasInitial) {
      throw new Error(`Step8: stateMachines[${i}] 缺起始状态(没有状态无入向转换)`);
    }
  });

  return { stateMachines: machines };
}

// =====================================================
// Step 9: UI
// =====================================================
export interface UIOutput {
  screens: Array<{
    id: string;
    name: string;
    dataFlow: string;
    bpmNodeId: string;
    fields: Array<{ name: string; type: string; required: boolean; controlType: string }>;
  }>;
}

export function guardUI(raw: any, dict?: DictOutput): UIOutput {
  ensureObject(raw, 'Step9 UI');

  const screens = ensureArray(raw.screens, 'screens');
  if (screens.length === 0) {
    throw new Error('Step9: screens 至少要有 1 个');
  }
  screens.forEach((s: any, i: number) => {
    ensureString(s.id, `screens[${i}].id`);
    ensureString(s.name, `screens[${i}].name`);
    ensureString(s.dataFlow, `screens[${i}].dataFlow`);
    ensureString(s.bpmNodeId, `screens[${i}].bpmNodeId`);

    const fields = ensureArray(s.fields, `screens[${i}].fields`);
    fields.forEach((f: any, fi: number) => {
      ensureString(f.name, `screens[${i}].fields[${fi}].name`);
      // 校验字段在 dict 里
      if (dict) {
        const dictFields = new Set([
          ...dict.elements.map((e: any) => e.name),
          ...dict.dataStores.flatMap((ds: any) => (ds.fields || []).map((ff: any) => ff.name)),
        ]);
        if (!dictFields.has(f.name)) {
          throw new Error(`Step9: screens[${i}].fields[${fi}].name "${f.name}" 不在 dict 中(LLM 幻觉!)`);
        }
      }
      ensureString(f.controlType, `screens[${i}].fields[${fi}].controlType`);
    });
  });

  return { screens };
}

  findExistingAnalysis, recordAnalysisRun,

=== FILE: ./sa-simple/src/llm.ts ===
// =====================================================
// LLM 调用 - 带 Retry(P1-1)
// 处理 502/429/网络临时错误,不处理格式错误(那是 guard 的事)
// =====================================================

export interface LLMConfig {
  apiKey: string;
  baseUrl: string;
  model: string;
  temperature?: number;
  maxTokens?: number;
}

let config: LLMConfig | null = null;

export function initLLM(c: LLMConfig): void {
  config = c;
}

/**
 * 调一次 LLM,带重试
 * @param systemPrompt 系统提示
 * @param userInput 用户输入(对象会 JSON.stringify)
 * @param retries 重试次数(默认 1,即总共 2 次)
 */
export async function callLLM(systemPrompt: string, userInput: any, retries: number = 1): Promise<any> {
  if (!config) throw new Error('LLM not initialized. Call initLLM() first.');

  const body = {
    model: config.model,
    messages: [
      { role: 'system', content: systemPrompt },
      { role: 'user', content: typeof userInput === 'string' ? userInput : JSON.stringify(userInput) },
    ],
    temperature: config.temperature ?? 0.3,
    max_tokens: config.maxTokens ?? 4096,
    response_format: { type: 'json_object' as const },
  };

  let lastError: Error | null = null;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt <= retries; attempt++) {
    try {
      // 30 秒超时(用 AbortController,Node 17+)
      const controller = new AbortController();
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), 30_000);

      try {
        const response = await fetch(`${config.baseUrl}/chat/completions`, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: {
            'Content-Type': 'application/json',
            'Authorization': `Bearer ${config.apiKey}`,
          },
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
          signal: controller.signal,
        });

        if (!response.ok) {
          // 5xx 或 429 = 临时错误,重试
          if (response.status >= 500 || response.status === 429) {
            throw new Error(`LLM ${response.status}: ${await response.text()}`);
          }
          // 4xx 其他 = 永久错误,不重试
          throw new Error(`LLM ${response.status}(永久错误,不重试): ${await response.text()}`);
        }

        const data: any = await response.json();
        const content = data.choices?.[0]?.message?.content;
        if (!content) throw new Error('LLM 返回 content 为空');

        // 智能 JSON 解析(剥 markdown code block 包裹)
        return JSON.parse(extractJson(content));
      } finally {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      lastError = e as Error;
      // AbortError(超时) → 重试
      // 5xx/429 → 重试
      // 4xx 其他 → 不重试
      const isRetryable =
        (e as Error).name === 'AbortError' ||
        /LLM 5\d\d|LLM 429/.test((e as Error).message);

      if (!isRetryable || attempt === retries) {
        throw e;
      }
      // 退避 1s, 2s
      const delay = 1000 * (attempt + 1);
      console.warn(`[LLM] 失败(将重试 ${attempt + 1}/${retries}):`, (e as Error).message);
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, delay));
    }
  }
  throw lastError;
}

function extractJson(text: string): string {
  const match = text.match(/```(?:json)?\s*([\s\S]*?)```/);
  if (match) return match[1].trim();
  return text.trim();
}

=== END FILE:  ===

// =====================================================
// SA 9 步 LLM Prompt 集中文件
// 每个 prompt 就是一段中文指令 + JSON Schema 例子
// AI 工程师可以直接复制到任意 LLM(GPT/Claude/DeepSeek)测试
//
// 版本规范(P1-2):
//   vMAJOR.MINOR.PATCH
//   - MAJOR: 输出结构大改(必须更新对应 guard)
//   - MINOR: 加了输出字段
//   - PATCH: 措辞微调
// =====================================================

// v1.0.0 - 初始版本
export const SCOPE_PROMPT = `你是 SA 流水线 Step 1 分析师,负责从客户需求文档提取结构化范围。

## 输入
客户原始需求文档。

## 任务
提取以下 3 类信息:

1. **系统边界** (systemBoundary)
   - inScope: 本次需要实现的功能
   - outOfScope: 明确不属于本次范围

2. **外部实体** (externalEntities)
   - 谁和系统交互(用户/系统/设备)
   - 每项含 name, type, description

3. **业务事件** (businessEvents)
   - 系统要响应的事
   - 每项含 id(从1递增), name, description, complexity
   - complexity 判定: simple=单步CRUD, medium=多步骤无分支, complex=有条件分支

## 输出格式(必须是合法 JSON)
{
  "systemBoundary": {
    "inScope": ["MES 报工系统", "工单管理"],
    "outOfScope": ["ERP 财务"]
  },
  "externalEntities": [
    {"name": "操作工", "type": "user", "description": "车间工人"}
  ],
  "businessEvents": [
    {"id": 1, "name": "工序报工", "description": "机加工序报工", "complexity": "complex"}
  ]
}`;

## 任务
生成分层数据流图(DFD)。

1. **contextDiagram**: 顶层 Context 图(整个系统作为一个 process)
2. **dfdLevels**: {level_0: [...], level_1: [...]} 多层分解
3. **processes**: 每个 process 含 id(P1/P1.1格式), name, inputFlows[], outputFlows[], parentId
4. **dataFlows**: [{name, fields[]}] - 数据流名和包含的字段
5. **dataStores**: [{name, fields[]}] - 数据存储名和字段

## 输出格式(必须是合法 JSON)

  "contextDiagram": {"process": "MES", "entities": ["worker"]},
  "dfdLevels": {
    "level_0": [{"id": "P1", "name": "录入与初核"}],
    "level_1": {"P1": [{"id": "P1.1", "name": "录入报工"}]}
  },
  "processes": [{"id": "P1.1", "name": "录入报工", "inputFlows": ["工单"], "outputFlows": ["报工数据"], "parentId": "P1"}],
  "dataFlows": [{"name": "工单", "fields": [{"name": "workOrderId", "type": "BIGINT"}]}],
  "dataStores": [{"name": "ProductionReport", "fields": [{"name": "id", "type": "BIGINT"}]}]
}`;

// v1.0.0 - 初始版本
export const BPM_PROMPT = `你是 SA 流水线 Step 3 业务流程分析师。

## 输入

## 任务

1. **swimLanes**: 泳道(角色/部门)
2. **activityNodes**: 活动节点,含 id, name, laneId, dfdProcessId, type(task/gateway/start/end)

=== FILE: ./sa-simple/src/runSA.ts ===
// =====================================================
// SA 简化版 9 步流水线 - 主入口(已加 3 层防御皮)
// 设计原则: 9 个顺序函数,每步 = LLM → guard → save
// 3 层防御皮:
//   P0-1 Schema Guard: LLM 输出必须过 guard 才能 save
//   P0-2 事务: 9 步全成功才提交,失败自动回滚
//   P0-3 幂等: 同样输入直接返回已有结果,不重跑 LLM
// =====================================================

import { createHash } from 'crypto';
import { callLLM } from './llm';
import {
  saveScope, saveDFD, saveBPM, saveDict, savePSpec,
  saveDecisionTable, saveER, saveStateMachine, saveUI,
  findExistingAnalysis, recordAnalysisRun, withTransaction,
} from './db';
import {
  SCOPE_PROMPT, DFD_PROMPT, BPM_PROMPT, DICT_PROMPT, PSPEC_PROMPT,
  DT_PROMPT, ER_PROMPT, STD_PROMPT, UI_PROMPT,
} from './prompts';
import {
  guardScope, guardDFD, guardBPM, guardDict, guardPSpec,
  guardDecisionTable, guardER, guardStateMachine, guardUI,
  ScopeOutput, DFDOutput, BPMOutput, DictOutput, PSpecOutput,
  DecisionTableOutput, EROutput, StateMachineOutput, UIOutput,
} from './guards';

export interface SARequest {
  tenantId: string;
  projectId: number;
  requirementText: string;
  userId: string;
}

export interface SAOutput {
  projectId: number;
  tenantId: string;
  reqHash: string;
  scopeId: number;
  dfdId: number;
  bpmId: number;
  dictId: number;
  pspecId: number;
  dtId: number;
  erId: number;
  stdId: number;
  uiId: number;
  scope: ScopeOutput;
  dfd: DFDOutput;
  bpm: BPMOutput;
  dict: DictOutput;
  pspec: PSpecOutput;
  decisionTable: DecisionTableOutput;
  er: EROutput;
  stateMachine: StateMachineOutput;
  ui: UIOutput;
}

// 求需求文本的 hash(用作幂等 key)
function getReqHash(text: string): string {
  return createHash('sha256').update(text).digest('hex').slice(0, 16);
}

/**
 * 主入口 - 9 步顺序执行,3 层防御皮包裹
 */
export async function runSA(req: SARequest): Promise<SAOutput> {
  const reqHash = getReqHash(req.requirementText);
  const start = Date.now();

  console.log(`[SA] 开始分析: project=${req.projectId} tenant=${req.tenantId} hash=${reqHash}`);

  // ==========================================
  // P0-3 幂等检查: 同样的需求文本,直接返回已有结果
  // ==========================================
  const existing = await findExistingAnalysis(req.tenantId, req.projectId, reqHash);
  if (existing) {
    console.log(`[SA] 幂等命中 hash=${reqHash},直接返回已有结果(跳过 LLM)`);
    return existing as SAOutput;
  }

  // ==========================================
  // P0-2 事务包装: 9 步全成功才提交,任何一步失败自动回滚
  // ==========================================
  try {
    return await withTransaction(async (trx) => {
      // ==========================================
      // Step 1: Scope
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 1/9: Scope (范围分析)`);
      const scope: ScopeOutput = guardScope(await callLLM(SCOPE_PROMPT, req.requirementText));
      const scopeId = await saveScope({
        tenantId: req.tenantId,
        projectId: req.projectId,
        systemBoundary: scope.systemBoundary,
        externalEntities: scope.externalEntities,
        businessEvents: scope.businessEvents,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ Scope saved id=${scopeId}, events=${scope.businessEvents.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 2: DFD
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 2/9: DFD (数据流图)`);
      const dfd: DFDOutput = guardDFD(await callLLM(DFD_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, scope,
      }));
      const dfdId = await saveDFD({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, scopeId,
        contextDiagram: dfd.contextDiagram, dfdLevels: dfd.dfdLevels,
        processes: dfd.processes, dataFlows: dfd.dataFlows, dataStores: dfd.dataStores,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ DFD saved id=${dfdId}, processes=${dfd.processes.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 3: BPM
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 3/9: BPM (业务流程图)`);
      const bpm: BPMOutput = guardBPM(await callLLM(BPM_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, scope, dfd,
      }));
      const bpmId = await saveBPM({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, dfdId,
        swimLanes: bpm.swimLanes, activityNodes: bpm.activityNodes,
        edges: bpm.edges, exceptionPaths: bpm.exceptionPaths,
        dfdProcessMappings: bpm.dfdProcessMappings,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ BPM saved id=${bpmId}, nodes=${bpm.activityNodes.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 4: Dict (★ 最关键 - 后面所有 Step 依赖它)
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 4/9: Dict (数据字典,关键)`);
      const dict: DictOutput = guardDict(await callLLM(DICT_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, scope, dfd, bpm,
      }));
      const dictId = await saveDict({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, dfdId, bpmId,
        elements: dict.elements, dataFlows: dict.dataFlows, dataStores: dict.dataStores,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ Dict saved id=${dictId}, fields=${dict.elements.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 5: PSPEC (校验字段在 Dict 里)
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 5/9: PSPEC (过程规格)`);
      const pspec: PSpecOutput = guardPSpec(await callLLM(PSPEC_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, dfd, dict,
      }), dict);  // 传 dict 用于字段引用校验
      const pspecId = await savePSpec({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, dictId, bpmId,
        processSpecs: pspec.processSpecs,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ PSPEC saved id=${pspecId}, specs=${pspec.processSpecs.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 6: DecisionTable
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 6/9: DecisionTable (业务规则)`);
      const decisionTable: DecisionTableOutput = guardDecisionTable(await callLLM(DT_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, dict, allDecisionTables: [],
      }));
      const dtId = await saveDecisionTable({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, pspecId, dictId,
        tables: decisionTable.tables,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ DecisionTable saved id=${dtId}, tables=${decisionTable.tables.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 7: ER
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 7/9: ER (实体关系)`);
      const er: EROutput = guardER(await callLLM(ER_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, scope, dfd, dict,
      }));
      const erId = await saveER({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, dictId,
        entities: er.entities, relationships: er.relationships,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ ER saved id=${erId}, entities=${er.entities.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 8: StateMachine
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 8/9: StateMachine (状态机)`);
      const stateMachine: StateMachineOutput = guardStateMachine(await callLLM(STD_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, dict, bpm,
      }));
      const stdId = await saveStateMachine({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, dictId, bpmId,
        stateMachines: stateMachine.stateMachines,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ StateMachine saved id=${stdId}, machines=${stateMachine.stateMachines.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // Step 9: UI (校验字段在 Dict 里)
      // ==========================================
      console.log(`[SA] Step 9/9: UI (UI 原型)`);
      const ui: UIOutput = guardUI(await callLLM(UI_PROMPT, {
        requirement: req.requirementText, dfd, dict, bpm, stateMachine,
      }), dict);  // 传 dict 用于字段引用校验
      const uiId = await saveUI({
        tenantId: req.tenantId, projectId: req.projectId, bpmId, dictId,
        screens: ui.screens,
      }, req.userId, trx);
      console.log(`[SA]   ✓ UI saved id=${uiId}, screens=${ui.screens.length}`);

      // ==========================================
      // 9 步全部成功,组装最终结果
      // ==========================================
      const result: SAOutput = {
        projectId: req.projectId,
        tenantId: req.tenantId,
        reqHash,
        scopeId, dfdId, bpmId, dictId, pspecId, dtId, erId, stdId, uiId,
        scope, dfd, bpm, dict, pspec, decisionTable, er, stateMachine, ui,
      };

      // 记录分析运行(下次同样 hash 直接命中)
      await recordAnalysisRun(req.tenantId, req.projectId, reqHash, result, 'COMPLETED', undefined, trx);

      console.log(`[SA] 分析完成: hash=${reqHash} 耗时=${Date.now() - start}ms`);
      return result;
    });
  } catch (e) {
    // 事务已自动回滚(全部 9 步都不会残留)
    console.error(`[SA] 分析失败: hash=${reqHash}`, (e as Error).message);

    // 失败也记录(便于排错,不在事务里)
    try {
      await recordAnalysisRun(req.tenantId, req.projectId, reqHash, {}, 'FAILED', (e as Error).message);
    } catch { /* ignore secondary error */ }

    throw e;
  }
}

=== END FILE:  ===

=== FILE: ./sa-simple/tests/runSA.test.ts ===
// 简化版 SA 测试 - 覆盖 3 层防御皮 + 9 步流水线 + 文档生成

import { generateMarkdownDoc } from '../src/document';
import {
  guardScope, guardDFD, guardDict, guardDecisionTable, guardER, guardStateMachine,
} from '../src/guards';

// Mock LLM 和 DB
jest.mock('../src/llm', () => ({
  callLLM: jest.fn(),
  initLLM: jest.fn(),
}));

jest.mock('../src/db', () => ({
  initDb: jest.fn(),
  getPool: jest.fn(),
  closeDb: jest.fn(),
  getFullSA: jest.fn(),
  withTransaction: jest.fn((fn) => fn({} as any)),  // 透传事务对象(实际跑 inline SQL)
  findExistingAnalysis: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(null),
  recordAnalysisRun: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(1),
  saveScope: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(1),
  saveDFD: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(2),
  saveBPM: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(3),
  saveDict: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(4),
  savePSpec: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(5),
  saveDecisionTable: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(6),
  saveER: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(7),
  saveStateMachine: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(8),
  saveUI: jest.fn().mockResolvedValue(9),
}));

import { callLLM } from '../src/llm';
import { runSA } from '../src/runSA';
import { findExistingAnalysis, recordAnalysisRun } from '../src/db';

const mockCallLLM = callLLM as jest.MockedFunction<typeof callLLM>;
const mockFindExisting = findExistingAnalysis as jest.MockedFunction<typeof findExistingAnalysis>;

beforeEach(() => {
  mockCallLLM.mockReset();
  mockFindExisting.mockReset();
  mockFindExisting.mockResolvedValue(null);
});

// 标准 9 步 LLM 输出(简化 mock,只给 guard 最少需要的字段)
const STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS = [
  {  // Step 1 Scope
    systemBoundary: { inScope: ['MES'], outOfScope: [] },
    externalEntities: [{ name: '工人', type: 'user', description: '' }],
    businessEvents: [{ id: 1, name: '工序报工', description: '', complexity: 'complex' }],
  },
  {  // Step 2 DFD
    contextDiagram: {},
    dfdLevels: { level_0: [], level_1: {} },
    processes: [{ id: 'P1.1', name: '录入报工', inputFlows: ['工单'], outputFlows: ['报工数据'], parentId: 'P1' }],
    dataFlows: [],
    dataStores: [],
  },
  {  // Step 3 BPM
    swimLanes: [{ laneId: 'L1', role: 'worker', name: '操作工' }],
    activityNodes: [{ id: 'N1', name: '录入', laneId: 'L1', dfdProcessId: 'P1.1', type: 'task' }],
    edges: [{ from: 'N1', to: 'END' }],
    exceptionPaths: [],
    dfdProcessMappings: { N1: 'P1.1' },
  },
  {  // Step 4 Dict (★ 需要 5 个审计字段)
    elements: [
      { name: 'Id', type: 'BIGINT', isPK: true },
      { name: 'TenantId', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' },
      { name: 'CreatedAt', type: 'DATETIME' },
      { name: 'CreatedBy', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' },
      { name: 'UpdatedAt', type: 'DATETIME' },
      { name: 'UpdatedBy', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' },
      { name: 'Qty', type: 'DECIMAL(18,2)' },
    ],
    dataFlows: [],
    dataStores: [{ name: 'ProductionReport', fields: [{ name: 'Id', type: 'BIGINT' }] }],
  },
  {  // Step 5 PSPEC
    processSpecs: [{ id: 'P1.1', name: '录入报工', input: ['Qty'], output: ['ProductionReport'] }],
  },
  {  // Step 6 DecisionTable
    tables: [{
      id: 'DT-test',
      conditions: [{ name: '报废率', operator: '>', value: 0.05 }],
      actions: [{ name: '合格' }, { name: '驳回' }],
      rules: [
        { conditionMask: [true], actionIndex: 0 },
        { conditionMask: [false], actionIndex: 1 },
      ],
    }],
  },
  {  // Step 7 ER
    entities: [{ name: 'ProductionReport', columns: [
      { name: 'id', type: 'BIGINT', isPK: true },
      { name: 'tenant_id', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' },
    ] }],
    relationships: [],
  },
  {  // Step 8 StateMachine
    stateMachines: [{
      entity: 'ProductionReport',
      states: ['Draft', 'Submitted', 'Rejected'],
      transitions: [
        { from: 'Draft', to: 'Submitted', trigger: 'Submit' },
        { from: 'Submitted', to: 'Rejected', trigger: 'Reject' },
      ],
    }],
  },
  {  // Step 9 UI
    screens: [{
      id: 'SCN-001', name: '报工录入', dataFlow: '工单', bpmNodeId: 'N1',
      fields: [{ name: 'Qty', type: 'DECIMAL(18,2)', required: true, controlType: 'number' }],
    }],
  },
];

beforeEach(() => {
  // 默认设 9 个 mock
  STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS.forEach(o => mockCallLLM.mockResolvedValueOnce(o));
});

describe('9 步流水线', () => {
  it('应该顺序调用 LLM 9 次', async () => {
    const result = await runSA({
      tenantId: 't1', projectId: 100,
      requirementText: 'MES 报工系统',
      userId: 'tester',
    });

    expect(mockCallLLM).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(9);
    expect(result.scopeId).toBe(1);
    expect(result.dictId).toBe(4);
    expect(result.uiId).toBe(9);
  });

  it('每步 LLM 输入应该包含上一步的输出(顺序依赖)', async () => {
    await runSA({
      tenantId: 't1', projectId: 100,
      requirementText: 'MES 报工系统',
      userId: 'tester',
    });

    // Step 2 (DFD) 输入 = requirement + scope
    expect(mockCallLLM.mock.calls[1][1]).toHaveProperty('scope');
    // Step 4 (Dict) 输入包含 dfd + bpm
    expect(mockCallLLM.mock.calls[3][1]).toHaveProperty('dfd');
    expect(mockCallLLM.mock.calls[3][1]).toHaveProperty('bpm');
    // Step 5 (PSPEC) 输入包含 dict
    expect(mockCallLLM.mock.calls[4][1]).toHaveProperty('dict');
  });
});

describe('P0-1 Schema Guard', () => {
  it('Step 1 Scope: 缺 businessEvents 应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardScope({
      systemBoundary: { inScope: [], outOfScope: [] },
      externalEntities: [],
    })).toThrow(/businessEvents/);
  });

  it('Step 1 Scope: complexity 取值错误应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardScope({
      systemBoundary: { inScope: [], outOfScope: [] },
      externalEntities: [],
      businessEvents: [{ id: 1, name: 'x', complexity: 'wrong' }],
    })).toThrow(/complexity/);
  });

  it('Step 2 DFD: 缺 processes 应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardDFD({
      contextDiagram: {}, dfdLevels: {}, dataFlows: [], dataStores: [],
    })).toThrow(/processes/);
  });

  it('Step 4 Dict: 类型不在白名单应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardDict({
      elements: [{ name: 'x', type: 'VARCHAR2(50)' },  // ❌ 不在白名单
        { name: 'TenantId', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' },
        { name: 'CreatedAt', type: 'DATETIME' },
        { name: 'CreatedBy', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' },
        { name: 'UpdatedAt', type: 'DATETIME' },
        { name: 'UpdatedBy', type: 'NVARCHAR(50)' }],
      dataFlows: [], dataStores: [],
    })).toThrow(/VARCHAR2/);
  });

  it('Step 4 Dict: 缺审计字段 TenantId 应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardDict({
      elements: [
        { name: 'Id', type: 'BIGINT' },
        // 没有 TenantId / CreatedAt / CreatedBy / UpdatedAt / UpdatedBy
      ],
      dataFlows: [], dataStores: [],
    })).toThrow(/TenantId/);
  });

  it('Step 6 DecisionTable: 缺默认规则应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardDecisionTable({
      tables: [{
        id: 'DT-1',
        conditions: [{ name: 'x', operator: '>', value: 1 }],
        actions: [{ name: 'A' }, { name: 'B' }],
        rules: [
          { conditionMask: [true], actionIndex: 0 },
          // 没有全 false 的默认规则
        ],
      }],
    })).toThrow(/默认规则/);
  });

  it('Step 7 ER: 重复实体名应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardER({
      entities: [
        { name: 'A', columns: [{ name: 'id', type: 'BIGINT' }] },
        { name: 'A', columns: [{ name: 'id', type: 'BIGINT' }] },  // 重复
      ],
      relationships: [],
    })).toThrow(/重复实体/);
  });

  it('Step 8 StateMachine: 缺起始状态应该抛错', () => {
    expect(() => guardStateMachine({
      stateMachines: [{
        entity: 'E',
        states: ['A', 'B'],
        transitions: [
          { from: 'A', to: 'B', trigger: 'go' },
          { from: 'B', to: 'A', trigger: 'back' },  // 形成环,两个状态都有入向
        ],
      }],
    })).toThrow(/起始状态/);
  });
});

describe('P0-2 事务包装', () => {
  it('任何一步失败,事务都不应该 commit(应该 throw)', async () => {
    // 让 Step 5 PSPEC 失败(LLM 返回无效)
    STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS.forEach(o => mockCallLLM.mockResolvedValueOnce(o));
    mockCallLLM.mockReset();
    STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS.slice(0, 4).forEach(o => mockCallLLM.mockResolvedValueOnce(o));
    // Step 5 模拟 LLM 返回无效 - 让 guard 抛错
    mockCallLLM.mockResolvedValueOnce({ processSpecs: 'not an array' as any });

    await expect(runSA({
      tenantId: 't1', projectId: 100,
      requirementText: 'MES', userId: 'tester',
    })).rejects.toThrow();

    // 失败后不应该 recordAnalysisRun COMPLETED,应该是 FAILED
    expect(recordAnalysisRun).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      expect.anything(), expect.anything(), expect.anything(),
      expect.anything(), 'FAILED', expect.any(String)
    );
  });
});

describe('P0-3 幂等性', () => {
  it('同样的需求文本应该直接返回已有结果(跳过 LLM)', async () => {
    const cachedResult = {
      projectId: 100, tenantId: 't1', reqHash: 'abc',
      scopeId: 1, dfdId: 2, bpmId: 3, dictId: 4, pspecId: 5, dtId: 6, erId: 7, stdId: 8, uiId: 9,
      scope: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[0] as any, dfd: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[1] as any,
      bpm: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[2] as any, dict: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[3] as any,
      pspec: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[4] as any, decisionTable: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[5] as any,
      er: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[6] as any, stateMachine: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[7] as any,
      ui: STANDARD_LLM_OUTPUTS[8] as any,
    };
    mockFindExisting.mockResolvedValueOnce(cachedResult as any);

    const result = await runSA({
      tenantId: 't1', projectId: 100,
      requirementText: 'MES', userId: 'tester',
    });

    expect(mockCallLLM).not.toHaveBeenCalled();  // 没调 LLM!
    expect(result.reqHash).toBe('abc');
    expect(result.scopeId).toBe(1);
  });

  it('不同的需求文本应该正常执行', async () => {
    // 第一次: 没找到缓存
    mockFindExisting.mockResolvedValueOnce(null);

    await runSA({
      tenantId: 't1', projectId: 100,
      requirementText: 'MES 报工',
      userId: 'tester',
    });

    expect(mockCallLLM).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(9);
  });
});

describe('P1-3 执行日志', () => {
  it('每步开始时应该 console.log', async () => {
    const logs: string[] = [];
    const origLog = console.log;
    console.log = (...args) => logs.push(args.join(' '));
    try {
      await runSA({
        tenantId: 't1', projectId: 100,
        requirementText: 'MES', userId: 'tester',
      });
    } finally {
      console.log = origLog;
    }

    expect(logs.some(l => l.includes('Step 1/9: Scope'))).toBe(true);
    expect(logs.some(l => l.includes('Step 9/9: UI'))).toBe(true);
    expect(logs.some(l => l.includes('分析完成'))).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe('Markdown 文档生成', () => {
  it('应该生成包含 9 个章节的文档', () => {
    const mockSA = {
      projectId: 100, tenantId: 't1',
      scope: { systemBoundary: { inScope: ['MES'] }, externalEntities: [], businessEvents: [] },
      dfd: { contextDiagram: {}, dfdLevels: {}, processes: [], dataFlows: [], dataStores: [] },
      bpm: { swimLanes: [], activityNodes: [] },
      dict: { elements: [], dataStores: [] },
      pspec: { processSpecs: [] },
      decisionTable: { tables: [] },
      er: { entities: [], relationships: [] },
      stateMachine: { stateMachines: [] },
      ui: { screens: [] },
    };
    const doc = generateMarkdownDoc(mockSA);

    expect(doc).toContain('## 1. 系统范围与边界');
    expect(doc).toContain('## 9. UI 原型');
  });
});
