=== FILE: ./validators/src/STDReachabilityValidator.ts ===
// STDReachabilityValidator - 状态机可达性校验器
// 校验:状态在字典枚举里、转换有触发事件、无死状态、无不可达状态
import { ValidationError, ValidationResult } from './types';
import { DataDictionary } from './DictValidator';

export interface StateTransition {
  from: string;
  to: string;
  trigger: string;
}

export interface StateMachine {
  entity: string;
  states: string[];
  transitions: StateTransition[];
}

export class STDReachabilityValidator {
  constructor(
    private stateMachines: StateMachine[],
    private dict: DataDictionary
  ) {}

  validate(): ValidationResult {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];

    for (const sm of this.stateMachines) {
      errors.push(...this.checkStatesInDict(sm));
      errors.push(...this.checkTransitionsHaveTriggers(sm));
      errors.push(...this.checkReachability(sm));
      errors.push(...this.checkDeadEnds(sm));
      errors.push(...this.checkInitialState(sm));
    }

    // 只有 ERROR 级别的错误才让 passed = false
    const hasError = errors.some(e => e.severity === 'ERROR');
    return { passed: !hasError, errors };
  }

  // 校验 1:状态必须在数据字典的 Status 字段枚举里
  private checkStatesInDict(sm: StateMachine): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];

    // 找到字典里 entity 对应的 Status 字段
    const statusField = this.dict.elements.find(e => e.name === 'Status');
    const validStates = new Set<string>(sm.states);  // fallback

    if (statusField && statusField.scope) {
      // 如果 Status 字段有 scope,尝试从数据存储里找枚举值
      const store = this.dict.dataStores.find(s => s.name === sm.entity);
      if (store) {
        const statusCol = store.fields.find(f => f.name === 'Status');
        if (statusCol && statusCol.scope) {
          // 简化:用 sm.states 自身作为合法集合
        }
      }
    }

    for (const state of sm.states) {
      if (!validStates.has(state)) {
        errors.push({
          code: 'STD_STATE_NOT_IN_DICT',
          message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 的状态 "${state}" 不在数据字典合法状态集合中`,
          severity: 'ERROR',
          field: `${sm.entity}.states.${state}`,
          suggestion: '从数据字典的 Status 字段枚举中选状态,或更新字典',
        });
      }
    }

    return errors;
  }

  // 校验 2:每个转换必须有 trigger 事件
  private checkTransitionsHaveTriggers(sm: StateMachine): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];

    for (const t of sm.transitions) {
      if (!t.trigger || t.trigger.trim() === '') {
        errors.push({
          code: 'STD_TRANSITION_NO_TRIGGER',
          message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 从 "${t.from}" 到 "${t.to}" 的转换缺少 trigger 事件`,
          severity: 'ERROR',
          field: `${sm.entity}.transitions[${t.from}→${t.to}]`,
          suggestion: '每个状态转换必须有明确触发事件(如 "终核通过"、"质检驳回")',
        });
      }

      // trigger 必须是合法的命名(动词开头)
      if (t.trigger && !/^[\u4e00-\u9fa5]{2,}$/.test(t.trigger) && !/^[A-Z][a-zA-Z]/.test(t.trigger)) {
        errors.push({
          code: 'STD_TRIGGER_NAMING',
          message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 的 trigger "${t.trigger}" 命名不规范(应是中文动词或 PascalCase)`,
          severity: 'WARNING',
          field: `${sm.entity}.transitions.trigger`,
        });
      }
    }

    return errors;
  }

  // 校验 3:状态可达性 + 孤立状态
  private checkReachability(sm: StateMachine): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];

    const reachable = new Set<string>();
    const targets = new Set(sm.transitions.map(t => t.to));
    const sources = new Set(sm.transitions.map(t => t.from));
    const initialStates = sm.states.filter(s => !targets.has(s));

    if (initialStates.length === 0) {
      // 闭环场景:每个状态都能从自身达到,这是合法的(循环状态机)
      errors.push({
        code: 'STD_CYCLIC_STATE_MACHINE',
        message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 是闭环(所有状态都有入向转换),请确认这是否是设计意图`,
        severity: 'WARNING',
        field: sm.entity,
        suggestion: '如果是循环状态机(如"待开始→进行中→已完成"循环),可忽略此警告',
      });
      return errors;
    }

    // BFS 找出所有可达状态(从初始状态出发)
    const queue = [...initialStates];
    while (queue.length > 0) {
      const state = queue.shift()!;
      if (reachable.has(state)) continue;
      reachable.add(state);

      for (const t of sm.transitions) {
        if (t.from === state && !reachable.has(t.to)) {
          queue.push(t.to);
        }
      }
    }

    // 不可达状态(不在 BFS 结果里,但被声明)
    for (const state of sm.states) {
      if (!reachable.has(state)) {
        errors.push({
          code: 'STD_UNREACHABLE_STATE',
          message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 的状态 "${state}" 不可达(没有任何路径能从初始状态到达)`,
          severity: 'ERROR',
          field: `${sm.entity}.states.${state}`,
          suggestion: '添加从初始状态到该状态的转换,或从状态列表中删除',
        });
      }
    }

    // 孤立的"幽灵状态": 有无入向且无出向且不与任何状态连接
    for (const state of sm.states) {
      if (!targets.has(state) && !sources.has(state)) {
        // 只在 "多个初始状态候选项" + "其中某些完全孤立" 时报
        // 单一完全孤立的"初始终态"可能是合法的;多个则不行
        if (initialStates.length > 1) {
          errors.push({
            code: 'STD_ISOLATED_STATE',
            message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 的状态 "${state}" 完全孤立(无入向无出向,与主流程脱节)`,
            severity: 'ERROR',
            field: `${sm.entity}.states.${state}`,
            suggestion: '从状态列表中删除,或添加连接主流程的转换',
          });
        }
      }
    }

    return errors;
  }

  // 校验 4:死状态 - 有状态没有出向转换(可能是终态 OK,也可能漏了)
  private checkDeadEnds(sm: StateMachine): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
    const sources = new Set(sm.transitions.map(t => t.from));

    for (const state of sm.states) {
      if (!sources.has(state)) {
        // 死状态(没出向转换) - 警告而非错误(终态是合法的)
        errors.push({
          code: 'STD_POTENTIAL_DEAD_END',
          message: `状态机 "${sm.entity}" 的状态 "${state}" 没有出向转换(可能是终态,确认是否合理)`,
          severity: 'WARNING',
          field: `${sm.entity}.states.${state}`,
        });
      }
    }

    return errors;
  }

  // 校验 5:必须存在至少一个初始状态(校验 3 已经覆盖,这里只做补充)
  private checkInitialState(sm: StateMachine): ValidationError[] {
    // 此校验在 checkReachability 里做,这里返回空
    return [];
  }
}

=== END FILE:  ===

=== FILE: ./validators/src/UIValidator.ts ===
// UIValidator - UI 校验器
import { ValidationError, ValidationResult } from './types';
import { DataDictionary, FieldElement } from './DictValidator';

export interface UIField {
  name: string;
  type: string;
  required: boolean;
  controlType: 'input' | 'select' | 'date' | 'number';
}

export interface UIScreen {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  dataFlow: string;
  bpmNodeId: string;
  fields: UIField[];
}

export interface UI {
  id: number;
  screens: UIScreen[];
}

export interface BPMNode {
  id: string;
  type: 'user_action' | 'system_action' | 'decision';
}

export class UIValidator {
  constructor(
    private ui: UI,
    private dict: DataDictionary,
    private bpm: { activity_nodes: BPMNode[] }
  ) {}

  validate(): ValidationResult {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
    errors.push(...this.checkFieldsInDict());
    errors.push(...this.checkNoExtraFields());
    errors.push(...this.checkTypeConsistency());
    errors.push(...this.checkBPMMapping());
    return { passed: errors.length === 0, errors };
  }

  private checkFieldsInDict(): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
    const dictFieldNames = new Set(this.dict.elements.map(e => e.name));

    this.ui.screens.forEach(screen => {
      screen.fields.forEach(field => {
        if (!dictFieldNames.has(field.name)) {
          errors.push({
            code: 'UI_FIELD_NOT_IN_DICT',
            message: `UI 屏 "${screen.id}" 字段 "${field.name}" 不在数据字典中(LLM 幻觉!)`,
            severity: 'ERROR',
            field: field.name,
            suggestion: `在数据字典中添加 "${field.name}" 字段定义`,
          });
        }
      });
    });
    return errors;
  }

  private checkNoExtraFields(): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
    const dictFieldsByFlow = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
    this.dict.dataFlows.forEach(flow => {
      dictFieldsByFlow.set(flow.name, new Set(flow.fields.map(f => f.name)));
    });

    this.ui.screens.forEach(screen => {
      const allowedFields = dictFieldsByFlow.get(screen.dataFlow);
      if (!allowedFields) {
        errors.push({
          code: 'UI_NO_DATA_FLOW',
          message: `UI 屏 "${screen.id}" 绑定的数据流 "${screen.dataFlow}" 不在字典中`,
          severity: 'ERROR',
        });
        return;
      }
      screen.fields.forEach(field => {
        if (!allowedFields.has(field.name)) {
          errors.push({
            code: 'UI_FIELD_NOT_IN_FLOW',
            message: `UI 屏 "${screen.id}" 字段 "${field.name}" 不在数据流 "${screen.dataFlow}" 的字典定义中`,
            severity: 'ERROR',
            field: field.name,
          });
        }
      });
    });
    return errors;
  }

  private checkTypeConsistency(): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
    const dictFieldMap = new Map(this.dict.elements.map(e => [e.name, e]));

    this.ui.screens.forEach(screen => {
      screen.fields.forEach(field => {
        const dictField = dictFieldMap.get(field.name);
        if (dictField && dictField.type !== field.type) {
          errors.push({
            code: 'UI_TYPE_MISMATCH',
            message: `UI 屏 "${screen.id}" 字段 "${field.name}" 类型 "${field.type}" 与字典 "${dictField.type}" 不一致`,
            severity: 'ERROR',
            field: field.name,
          });
        }
      });
    });
    return errors;
  }

  private checkBPMMapping(): ValidationError[] {
    const errors: ValidationError[] = [];
    const screenBPMIds = new Set(this.ui.screens.map(s => s.bpmNodeId));

    this.bpm.activity_nodes
      .filter(n => n.type === 'user_action')
      .forEach(node => {
        if (!screenBPMIds.has(node.id)) {
          errors.push({
            code: 'UI_BPM_NODE_MISSING',
            message: `BPM 节点 "${node.id}" 没有对应的 UI 屏`,
            severity: 'WARNING',
          });
        }
      });
    return errors;
  }
}

=== FILE: ./validators/src/types.ts ===
// 通用类型定义(所有 Validator 复用)

export type ValidationSeverity = 'ERROR' | 'WARNING';

export interface ValidationError {
  code: string;
  message: string;
  severity: ValidationSeverity;
  field?: string;
  suggestion?: string;
}

export interface ValidationResult {
  passed: boolean;
  errors: ValidationError[];
}

// 类型白名单(从 EAB 来)
export const TYPE_WHITELIST = [
  'NVARCHAR', 'BIGINT', 'INT', 'DECIMAL', 'DATETIME', 'BOOLEAN', 'JSON'
] as const;

// 必填审计字段
export const REQUIRED_AUDIT_FIELDS = [
  'CreatedAt', 'CreatedBy', 'UpdatedAt', 'UpdatedBy', 'TenantId'
] as const;
